# 📱 Hapto Design System

> **Design system focado em ergonomia mobile, acessibilidade tátil e fundamentos de interface.**  
> Mais do que copiar código: uma ferramenta viva para entender a anatomia, o alcance do polegar e as decisões por trás de cada componente.

🔗 **Acesse online:** [https://hapto.vercel.app](https://hapto.vercel.app)

---

## 🎯 Sobre o Projeto

O **Hapto** foi concebido para resolver um dos maiores problemas das interfaces modernas: componentes pensados para mouse e desktop que são simplesmente adaptados para telas móveis sem respeitar a biomecânica da mão humana.

Através de uma abordagem baseada em **Atomic Design**, diretrizes da **WCAG 2.2** e padrões de ergonomia do **iOS Human Interface Guidelines** e **Material Design**, o Hapto oferece:

1. **Alvos Táteis Calibrados (Touch Targets)**: Garantia do padrão ouro de 48dp (e mínimo Apple de 44pt) para prevenir erros de digitação e toques involuntários.
2. **Thumb Zone First**: Componentes estruturados para a zona de alcance natural do polegar, minimizando esforço articular.
3. **Contraste Sob Luz Solar**: Paleta de cores testada contra as taxas de contraste WCAG (4.5:1 para texto normal, 3:1 para alvos gráficos).
4. **Prevenção de Zoom Involuntário**: Escala tipográfica calibrada (mínimo de 16px em inputs) para evitar comportamento indesejado no iOS Safari.

---

## 🧩 Módulos da Aplicação

- **🌐 [Landing Page](/)**: Apresentação da filosofia, demonstração interativa do aparelho móvel e visão geral dos pilares.
- **📚 [Fundamentos](/fundamentals)**:
  - Decomposição em **Atomic Architecture** (Tokens, Átomos, Moléculas, Organismos).
  - Pilares ergonômicos e heurísticas visuais.
  - Cheat sheet com regras de espaçamento, respiro (8dp+) e tamanhos.
- **📦 [Catálogo de Componentes](/components)**:
  - Vitrine completa com busca instantânea e filtros por camada atômica.
  - Demonstração interativa, código-fonte pronto para uso e explicação da ergonomia por trás de cada variação.
- **🧪 [Playground Ergonômico](/playground)**:
  - Simulador de alcance do polegar (*Thumb Zone*).
  - Alternância entre pegada com **mão direita** e **mão esquerda**.
  - Diagnóstico tátil em tempo real com mapeamento das zonas: *Natural*, *Esticada* e *Difícil*.
  - Cenários reais: Feed de Notícias, Formulário e Ações/FAB.

---

## 🛠️ Componentes Disponíveis

| Categoria | Componentes |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Tokens** | Cores & Contraste WCAG, Tipografia & Escala, Espaçamentos & Targets |
| **Átomos** | Button, FAB, IconButton, Input, Switch, Checkbox, Badge, Chip, Ícones |
| **Moléculas** | SearchBar / SearchInput, Informative Cards, SwitchRow, CheckboxRow, SegmentedControl |
| **Organismos** | BottomNav (Thumb Zone), TopAppBar, Feedback (Alertas & Snackbars) |

---

## 🚀 Tecnologias Utilizadas

- **Framework**: [Next.js](https://nextjs.org) (App Router, Turbopack)
- **Linguagem**: [TypeScript](https://www.typescriptlang.org)
- **Biblioteca de UI**: [React 19](https://react.dev)
- **Estilização**: [Tailwind CSS v4](https://tailwindcss.com) + [class-variance-authority](https://cva.style/docs)
- **Animações**: [Motion](https://motion.dev) (Framer Motion)
- **Ícones**: [Lucide React](https://lucide.dev)
- **Temas**: [next-themes](https://github.com/pacocoursey/next-themes) (Dark/Light mode)

---

## 💻 Como Executar Localmente

### Pré-requisitos
- [Node.js](https://nodejs.org) (versão 20 ou superior)
- `npm`, `yarn`, `pnpm` ou `bun`

### Passo a Passo

1. **Clone o repositório:**
   ```bash
   git clone https://github.com/whoisnathaliamelo/hapto.git
   cd hapto
   ```

2. **Instale as dependências:**
   ```bash
   npm install
   ```

3. **Inicie o servidor de desenvolvimento:**
   ```bash
   npm run dev
   ```

4. **Abra no navegador:**
   Acesse [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000)

### Scripts Disponíveis

- `npm run dev`: Inicia o ambiente de desenvolvimento.
- `npm run build`: Cria a versão de produção otimizada.
- `npm run start`: Inicia o servidor com o build de produção.
- `npm run lint`: Executa a verificação estática de código com o ESLint.

---

## 📐 Estrutura de Pastas

```text
hapto/
├── app/                  # Rotas e layouts do Next.js (App Router)
│   ├── components/       # Página do Catálogo de Componentes
│   ├── fundamentals/     # Página de Fundamentos & Arquitetura Atômica
│   ├── playground/       # Página do Laboratório/Simulador Interativo
│   └── page.tsx          # Landing Page
├── components/
│   ├── catalog/          # Componentes visuais do catálogo (showcases, filtros)
│   ├── fundamentals/     # Seções da página de fundamentos
│   ├── landing/          # Seções da landing page
│   ├── playground/       # Simulador do aparelho, controles e mapa de calor
│   └── ui/               # Biblioteca de componentes do Design System
├── lib/                  # Utilitários (cn, formatação, etc.)
└── public/               # Arquivos estáticos
```

---

ARQUIVO: components/catalog/catalog-search.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import { SearchX, RotateCcw } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { SearchInput } from "@/components/ui";

interface CatalogSearchProps {
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  resultCount: number;
  totalCount: number;
}

const QUICK_SUGGESTIONS = [
  "WCAG",
  "Espaçamento",
  "Botões",
  "Inputs",
  "Switches",
  "Bottom Nav",
];

function getIsMac() {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return false;
  return /(Mac|iPhone|iPod|iPad)/i.test(navigator.platform || "");
}

export function CatalogSearch({
  value,
  onChange,
  resultCount,
  totalCount,
}: CatalogSearchProps) {
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const isMac = useSyncExternalStore(
    () => () => {},
    getIsMac,
    () => false
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "k") {
        e.preventDefault();
        inputRef.current?.focus();
        inputRef.current?.select();
        return;
      }

      if (
        e.key === "/" &&
        document.activeElement?.tagName !== "INPUT" &&
        document.activeElement?.tagName !== "TEXTAREA"
      ) {
        e.preventDefault();
        inputRef.current?.focus();
        inputRef.current?.select();
        return;
      }

      if (e.key === "Escape" && document.activeElement === inputRef.current) {
        if (value) {
          onChange("");
        } else {
          inputRef.current?.blur();
        }
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  }, [value, onChange]);

  const handleClear = () => {
    onChange("");
    inputRef.current?.focus();
  };

  const isSearching = value.trim().length > 0;

  return (
    <div className="w-full space-y-3">
      <SearchInput
        inputRef={inputRef}
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        onClear={handleClear}
        variant="glass"
        placeholder="Pesquisar componentes, tokens ou ergonomia (ex: botões, inputs, wcag)..."
        aria-label="Pesquisar componentes no catálogo"
        className={cn(
          "rounded-2xl",
          isSearching && "border-primary/60 ring-2 ring-primary/15"
        )}
        endAdornment={
          isSearching ? (
            <span className="hidden sm:inline-flex rounded-full bg-primary/10 border border-primary/20 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-primary">
              {resultCount} {resultCount === 1 ? "resultado" : "resultados"}
            </span>
          ) : (
            <kbd
              title={`Pressione ${isMac ? "⌘K" : "Ctrl+K"} ou / para focar`}
              onClick={() => inputRef.current?.focus()}
              className="hidden sm:inline-flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface-raised px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-muted shadow-2xs hover:text-ink transition-colors"
            >
              <span>{isMac ? "⌘" : "Ctrl"}</span>
              <span>K</span>
            </kbd>
          )
        }
      />

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 px-1 text-xs text-muted">
        <span className="font-medium mr-1 hidden sm:inline">Sugestões:</span>
        {QUICK_SUGGESTIONS.map((suggestion) => {
          const isSelected = value.toLowerCase() === suggestion.toLowerCase();
          return (
            <button
              key={suggestion}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onChange(isSelected ? "" : suggestion)}
              className={cn(
                "min-h-7 rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1 font-medium transition-all duration-150 active:scale-95 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary",
                isSelected
                  ? "border-primary bg-primary text-on-primary font-semibold shadow-2xs"
                  : "border-line/70 bg-surface/60 hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-surface hover:text-ink"
              )}
            >
              {suggestion}
            </button>
          );
        })}

        {isSearching && (
          <span className="sm:hidden ml-auto font-medium text-primary">
            {resultCount}/{totalCount}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

interface CatalogSearchEmptyStateProps {
  query: string;
  onClear: () => void;
}

export function CatalogSearchEmptyState({
  query,
  onClear,
}: CatalogSearchEmptyStateProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-line/80 bg-surface/50 p-8 sm:p-12 text-center backdrop-blur-xs">
      <div className="flex size-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface-raised text-muted shadow-2xs mb-4">
        <SearchX className="size-7 text-muted" />
      </div>

      <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-ink sm:text-xl">
        Nenhum componente encontrado
      </h3>

      <p className="mt-2 max-w-md text-sm text-muted leading-relaxed">
        Não encontramos nenhum componente ou token correspondente a{" "}
        <span className="font-semibold text-ink">&ldquo;{query}&rdquo;</span>. Tente buscar
        por termos como alvos táteis, botões, inputs, espaçamento ou navegação.
      </p>

      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onClear}
        className="mt-6 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-primary px-5 text-sm font-semibold text-on-primary shadow-xs transition-all duration-200 hover:bg-primary-hover active:scale-[0.98] focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-primary"
      >
        <RotateCcw className="size-4" />
        <span>Limpar busca e ver todos</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/catalog/catalog-view.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
import { Reveal } from "@/components/reveal";
import { FilterSidebar } from "./filter-sidebar";
import { MobileFilterChips } from "./mobile-filter-chips";
import { UsageGuide } from "./usage-guide";
import { CatalogSearch, CatalogSearchEmptyState } from "./catalog-search";
import {
  TokensGroup,
  AtomsGroup,
  MoleculesGroup,
  OrganismsGroup,
} from "./groups";
import { SECTIONS, GROUPS, CATEGORY_LABELS, normalizeSearch } from "./constants";
import type { AtomicCategory, ComponentSection } from "./types";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowLeft } from "lucide-react";

export function CatalogView() {
  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState<AtomicCategory>("all");
  const [activeSection, setActiveSection] = useState<ComponentSection>("all");
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");

  const isSearching = searchQuery.trim().length > 0;

  const visibleSections = useMemo(() => {
    if (!isSearching) return undefined;
    const query = normalizeSearch(searchQuery);

    const matches = SECTIONS.filter((section) => {
      if (normalizeSearch(section.label).includes(query)) return true;
      if (normalizeSearch(section.id).includes(query)) return true;
      if (normalizeSearch(CATEGORY_LABELS[section.category]).includes(query)) return true;
      if (section.description && normalizeSearch(section.description).includes(query)) return true;
      if (section.keywords?.some((kw) => normalizeSearch(kw).includes(query))) return true;
      return false;
    });

    return new Set<ComponentSection>(matches.map((s) => s.id));
  }, [searchQuery, isSearching]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleHash = () => {
      const rawHash = window.location.hash.replace(/^#/, "");
      if (!rawHash) return;

      const clean = rawHash.replace(/^section-/, "");

      const matchedGroup = GROUPS.find((g) => g.id === clean);
      if (matchedGroup) {
        setActiveCategory(matchedGroup.id);
        setActiveSection("all");
        setTimeout(() => {
          const el = document.getElementById(`${matchedGroup.id}-group`);
          if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
        }, 100);
        return;
      }

      const matchedSection = SECTIONS.find((s) => s.id === clean);
      if (matchedSection) {
        setActiveCategory(matchedSection.category);
        setActiveSection(matchedSection.id);
        setTimeout(() => {
          const el = document.getElementById(matchedSection.id);
          if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
        }, 120);
      }
    };

    handleHash();
    window.addEventListener("hashchange", handleHash);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("hashchange", handleHash);
  }, []);

  const handleSelect = (category: AtomicCategory, section: ComponentSection) => {
    setActiveCategory(category);
    setActiveSection(section);

    const hash =
      section !== "all"
        ? `#${section}`
        : category !== "all"
        ? `#${category}`
        : "";

    if (hash) {
      window.history.pushState(null, "", hash);
      setTimeout(() => {
        const targetId = section !== "all" ? section : `${category}-group`;
        const el = document.getElementById(targetId);
        if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
      }, 60);
    } else {
      window.history.pushState(null, "", window.location.pathname);
    }
  };

  const showGroup = (category: Exclude<AtomicCategory, "all">) => {
    if (visibleSections) {
      return SECTIONS.some(
        (s) => s.category === category && visibleSections.has(s.id)
      );
    }
    const matchesCategory = activeCategory === "all" || activeCategory === category;
    const matchesSection =
      activeSection === "all" ||
      SECTIONS.some((s) => s.category === category && s.id === activeSection);
    return matchesCategory && matchesSection;
  };

  const hasNoResults = visibleSections && visibleSections.size === 0;

  return (
    <section className="relative isolate overflow-clip border-b border-line/40">
      <div className="bg-dots absolute inset-0 -z-10 opacity-70 [mask-image:radial-gradient(ellipse_at_top,black,transparent_60%)]" />
      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-1/2 -z-10 h-72 w-full max-w-4xl -translate-x-1/2 bg-radial from-primary/15 via-transparent to-transparent opacity-60 blur-2xl" />

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 xl:px-12 pt-6 sm:pt-8 pb-20 lg:pb-24">
        <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-start">
          <Link
            href="/"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-line bg-surface/80 px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-muted backdrop-blur-sm transition-all hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink active:scale-95 shadow-2xs"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="size-3.5 text-primary" />
            <span>Voltar ao Início</span>
          </Link>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-start gap-8 lg:gap-10 xl:gap-14">
          <FilterSidebar
            activeCategory={activeCategory}
            activeSection={activeSection}
            visibleSections={visibleSections}
            onSelect={handleSelect}
          />

          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 flex flex-col items-center">
            <div className="w-full max-w-4xl xl:max-w-5xl space-y-10 lg:space-y-14">
              <header className="mx-auto max-w-2xl text-center">
                <Reveal>
                  <div className="mb-3 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-line/80 bg-surface/80 px-3.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-muted shadow-2xs backdrop-blur-xs">
                    <span className="size-2 rounded-full bg-primary animate-pulse" />
                    <span>Design System Mobile-First</span>
                  </div>

                  <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-ink sm:text-4xl lg:text-5xl lg:leading-[1.15]">
                    Catálogo de{" "}
                    <span className="text-primary underline decoration-primary/30 underline-offset-8">
                      Componentes
                    </span>
                  </h1>
                  <p className="mt-4 text-base leading-relaxed text-muted sm:text-lg">
                    Biblioteca modular guiada por Atomic Design e calibrada para ergonomia móvel.
                  </p>
                </Reveal>
              </header>

              <Reveal delay={0.05} className="w-full">
                <CatalogSearch
                  value={searchQuery}
                  onChange={setSearchQuery}
                  resultCount={visibleSections ? visibleSections.size : SECTIONS.length}
                  totalCount={SECTIONS.length}
                />
              </Reveal>

              {activeCategory === "all" && activeSection === "all" && !isSearching && (
                <UsageGuide />
              )}

              <Reveal delay={0.1} className="lg:hidden w-full">
                <MobileFilterChips
                  activeCategory={activeCategory}
                  activeSection={activeSection}
                  visibleSections={visibleSections}
                  onSelect={handleSelect}
                />
              </Reveal>

              {hasNoResults ? (
                <CatalogSearchEmptyState
                  query={searchQuery}
                  onClear={() => setSearchQuery("")}
                />
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-14 lg:space-y-16">
                  {showGroup("tokens") && (
                    <TokensGroup
                      activeSection={activeSection}
                      visibleSections={visibleSections}
                    />
                  )}
                  {showGroup("atoms") && (
                    <AtomsGroup
                      activeSection={activeSection}
                      visibleSections={visibleSections}
                    />
                  )}
                  {showGroup("molecules") && (
                    <MoleculesGroup
                      activeSection={activeSection}
                      visibleSections={visibleSections}
                    />
                  )}
                  {showGroup("organisms") && (
                    <OrganismsGroup
                      activeSection={activeSection}
                      visibleSections={visibleSections}
                    />
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

  },
];

export const SECTION_ICONS: Record<Exclude<ComponentSection, "all">, LucideIcon> = {
  colors: Palette,
  typography: Type,
  spacing: MoveVertical,
  buttons: MousePointerClick,
  inputs: TextCursorInput,
  switches: ToggleRight,
  icons: Shapes,
  search: Search,
  cards: IdCard,
  "bottom-nav": PanelBottom,
  "top-bar": PanelTop,
  feedback: Bell,
};

export const CATEGORY_LABELS: Record<SectionMeta["category"], string> = {
  tokens: "Token",
  atoms: "Átomo",
  molecules: "Molécula",
  organisms: "Organismo",
};

export const GROUPS: { id: Exclude<AtomicCategory, "all">; label: string }[] = [
  { id: "tokens", label: "Tokens" },
  { id: "atoms", label: "Átomos" },
  { id: "molecules", label: "Moléculas" },
  { id: "organisms", label: "Organismos" },
];

export const shortLabel = (label: string) => label.replace(/\s*\(.*\)/, "");

export function normalizeSearch(str: string): string {
  return str
    .toLowerCase()
    .normalize("NFD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
    .trim();
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/catalog/filter-sidebar.tsx
================================================================================

import { LayoutGrid } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { GROUPS, SECTION_ICONS, SECTIONS, shortLabel } from "./constants";
import type { AtomicCategory, ComponentSection } from "./types";

interface FilterSidebarProps {
  activeCategory: AtomicCategory;
  activeSection: ComponentSection;
  visibleSections?: Set<ComponentSection>;
  onSelect: (category: AtomicCategory, section: ComponentSection) => void;
}

export function FilterSidebar({
  activeCategory,
  activeSection,
  visibleSections,
  onSelect,
}: FilterSidebarProps) {
  const isAllActive = activeCategory === "all" && activeSection === "all";
  const totalDisplayCount = visibleSections ? visibleSections.size : SECTIONS.length;

  return (
    <aside className="hidden lg:block lg:sticky lg:top-24 lg:self-start w-64 xl:w-72 shrink-0">
      <nav
        aria-label="Filtro de componentes"
        className="max-h-[calc(100svh-7.5rem)] overflow-y-auto pr-2 [scrollbar-width:thin] [scrollbar-color:var(--line)_transparent]"
      >
        <div className="mb-3 px-1">
          <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
            Explorar Catálogo
          </span>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          aria-pressed={isAllActive}
          onClick={() => onSelect("all", "all")}
          className={cn(
            "flex min-h-11 w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 text-sm transition-all duration-200 active:scale-[0.98] focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-primary",
            isAllActive
              ? "bg-primary text-on-primary font-semibold shadow-sm"
              : "border border-line bg-surface font-medium text-ink hover:border-primary/50 hover:text-primary"
          )}
        >
          <LayoutGrid className="size-4 shrink-0" />
          <span className="flex-1 text-left">Todos os componentes</span>
          <span
            className={cn(
              "rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold",
              isAllActive ? "bg-on-primary/20 text-on-primary" : "bg-surface-raised text-muted"
            )}
          >
            {totalDisplayCount}
          </span>
        </button>

        {GROUPS.map((group) => {
          const items = SECTIONS.filter((item) => item.category === group.id);
          const matchingItems = items.filter(
            (item) => !visibleSections || visibleSections.has(item.id)
          );
          const isGroupActive = activeCategory === group.id && activeSection === "all";

          if (visibleSections && matchingItems.length === 0) {
            return null;
          }

          return (
            <div key={group.id} className="mt-6">
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={isGroupActive}
                onClick={() => onSelect(group.id, "all")}
                className={cn(
                  "flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-lg px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-primary",
                  isGroupActive ? "text-primary" : "text-muted hover:text-primary"
                )}
              >
                <span>{group.label}</span>
                <span className="rounded-full border border-line/60 bg-surface-raised px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] tracking-normal">
                  {matchingItems.length}
                </span>
              </button>

              <ul className="mt-2 ml-3 border-l border-line">
                {items.map((item) => {
                  const isVisible = !visibleSections || visibleSections.has(item.id);
                  if (visibleSections && !isVisible) return null;

                  const Icon = SECTION_ICONS[item.id as Exclude<ComponentSection, "all">];
                  const isActive = activeSection === item.id;
                  return (
                    <li key={item.id}>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        aria-pressed={isActive}
                        title={item.label}
                        onClick={() => onSelect(item.category, item.id)}
                        className={cn(
                          "relative flex min-h-11 w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-r-xl pr-3 pl-4 text-left text-sm leading-snug transition-all duration-200 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-primary",
                          isActive
                            ? "bg-primary-light font-semibold text-primary before:absolute before:inset-y-1 before:-left-px before:w-0.5 before:rounded-full before:bg-primary"
                            : "text-muted hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink"
                        )}
                      >
                        <Icon className="size-4 shrink-0" />
                        <span>{shortLabel(item.label)}</span>
                      </button>
                    </li>
                  );
                })}
              </ul>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </nav>
    </aside>
  );
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/catalog/group-heading.tsx

import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";

interface GroupHeadingProps {
  icon: LucideIcon;
  title: string;
  count: number;
}

export function GroupHeading({ icon: Icon, title, count }: GroupHeadingProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 border-b border-line/40 pb-4 text-center">
      <div className="flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-primary-light text-primary shadow-2xs">
        <Icon className="size-4.5" />
      </div>
      <h2 className="text-2xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-ink">{title}</h2>
      <span className="rounded-full border border-line/60 bg-surface-raised px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
        {count} {count === 1 ? "componente" : "componentes"}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Blocks } from "lucide-react";
import { GroupHeading } from "../group-heading";
import { ShowcaseCard } from "../showcase-card";
import { SECTIONS } from "../constants";
import type { CatalogGroupProps, ComponentSection } from "../types";
import {
  ButtonsShowcase,
  BUTTONS_CODE,
  InputsShowcase,
  INPUTS_CODE,
  SwitchesShowcase,
  SWITCHES_CODE,
  IconsShowcase,
  ICONS_CODE,
} from "../showcases";

export function AtomsGroup({ activeSection, visibleSections }: CatalogGroupProps) {
  const shouldRender = (id: ComponentSection) => {
    const matchesActive = activeSection === "all" || activeSection === id;
    const matchesSearch = !visibleSections || visibleSections.has(id);
    return matchesActive && matchesSearch;
  };

  const count = SECTIONS.filter(
    (s) => s.category === "atoms" && shouldRender(s.id)
  ).length;

  if (visibleSections && count === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <section id="atoms-group" className="scroll-mt-24">
      <GroupHeading icon={Blocks} title="Átomos" count={count} />

      <div className="mt-8 grid gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2">
        {shouldRender("buttons") && (
          <ShowcaseCard
            id="buttons"
            section="buttons"
            title="Botões (Normal, Secundário, FAB)"
            description="Alvos táteis calibrados entre 40px e 56px para polegar."
            code={BUTTONS_CODE}
            delay={0}
          >
            <ButtonsShowcase />
          </ShowcaseCard>
        )}

        {shouldRender("inputs") && (
          <ShowcaseCard
            id="inputs"
            section="inputs"
            title="Inputs de Texto"
            description="Campos com altura de 48dp e tipografia mínima que evita zoom involuntário."
            code={INPUTS_CODE}
            delay={0.08}
          >
            <InputsShowcase />
          </ShowcaseCard>
        )}

        {shouldRender("switches") && (
          <ShowcaseCard
            id="switches"
            section="switches"
            title="Switches e Checkboxes"
            description="Controles binários com estados visuais claros e feedback tátil."
            code={SWITCHES_CODE}
            delay={0.16}
          >
            <SwitchesShowcase />
          </ShowcaseCard>
        )}

        {shouldRender("icons") && (
          <ShowcaseCard
            id="icons"

            title="Ícones Ergonômicos"
            description="Metáforas visuais com grid de toque acessível."
            code={ICONS_CODE}
            delay={0.24}
          >
            <IconsShowcase />
          </ShowcaseCard>
        )}
      </div>

  );
}

"use client";

import { Palette } from "lucide-react";
import { GroupHeading } from "../group-heading";
import { ShowcaseCard } from "../showcase-card";
import { SECTIONS } from "../constants";
import type { CatalogGroupProps, ComponentSection } from "../types";
import {
  ColorsShowcase,
  COLORS_CODE,
  TypographyShowcase,
  TYPOGRAPHY_CODE,
  SpacingShowcase,
  SPACING_CODE,
} from "../showcases";

export function TokensGroup({ activeSection, visibleSections }: CatalogGroupProps) {
  const shouldRender = (id: ComponentSection) => {
    const matchesActive = activeSection === "all" || activeSection === id;
    const matchesSearch = !visibleSections || visibleSections.has(id);
    return matchesActive && matchesSearch;
  };

  const count = SECTIONS.filter(
    (s) => s.category === "tokens" && shouldRender(s.id)
  ).length;

  if (visibleSections && count === 0) {

  }

  return (

            id="colors"

            delay={0}
          >
            <ColorsShowcase />
          </ShowcaseCard>
        )}

        {shouldRender("typography") && (
          <ShowcaseCard
            id="typography"
            section="typography"

            delay={0.16}
          >
            <SpacingShowcase />
          </ShowcaseCard>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/catalog/mobile-filter-chips.tsx
================================================================================

import { FilterChip } from "@/components/ui";
import { SECTIONS, shortLabel } from "./constants";
import type { AtomicCategory, ComponentSection } from "./types";

interface MobileFilterChipsProps {
  activeCategory: AtomicCategory;
  activeSection: ComponentSection;
  visibleSections?: Set<ComponentSection>;
  onSelect: (category: AtomicCategory, section: ComponentSection) => void;
}

export function MobileFilterChips({
  activeCategory,
  activeSection,
  visibleSections,
  onSelect,
}: MobileFilterChipsProps) {
  const isAllActive = activeCategory === "all" && activeSection === "all";

  const displayedSections = visibleSections
    ? SECTIONS.filter((s) => visibleSections.has(s.id))
    : SECTIONS;

  return (
    <nav aria-label="Filtro de componentes (mobile)" className="-mx-6 overflow-x-auto px-6">
      <ul className="flex gap-2 pb-2">
        {[{ id: "all" as const, label: "Todos", category: "all" as const }, ...displayedSections].map((item) => {
          const isActive = item.id === "all" ? isAllActive : activeSection === item.id;
          return (
            <li key={item.id} className="shrink-0">
              <FilterChip
                selected={isActive}
                size="lg"
                onClick={() => onSelect(item.category, item.id)}
              >
                {item.id === "all" ? item.label : shortLabel(item.label)}
              </FilterChip>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
    </nav>
  );
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/catalog/showcase-card.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Check, Code2, Copy, Eye } from "lucide-react";
import { Reveal } from "@/components/reveal";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { CATEGORY_LABELS, SECTION_ICONS, SECTIONS } from "./constants";
import type { ComponentSection } from "./types";

interface ShowcaseCardProps {
  id: string;
  section: Exclude<ComponentSection, "all">;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
  placeholder?: string;
  delay: number;
  code?: string;
}

export function ShowcaseCard({
  id,
  section,
  title,
  description,
  children,
  placeholder,
  delay,
  code,
}: ShowcaseCardProps) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<"preview" | "code">("preview");
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const Icon = SECTION_ICONS[section];
  const meta = SECTIONS.find((item) => item.id === section);

  const handleCopy = async () => {
    if (!code) return;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(code);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Falha ao copiar:", err);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Reveal delay={delay} className="h-full">
      <article
        id={id}
        className="group flex h-full scroll-mt-24 flex-col rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-6 shadow-xs transition-all hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-primary/50 hover:shadow-md"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-primary-light text-primary">
            {Icon && <Icon className="size-5" />}
          </div>
          {meta && (
            <span className="rounded-full bg-surface-raised px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-muted">
              {CATEGORY_LABELS[meta.category]}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        <h3 className="mt-5 text-base font-bold text-ink transition-colors group-hover:text-primary">
          {title}
        </h3>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted min-h-[34px]">{description}</p>

        {code && (
          <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line/60 pb-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-surface-raised p-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveTab("preview")}
                className={cn(
                  "flex min-h-9 md:min-h-7 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-3 md:px-2.5 py-1.5 md:py-1 text-xs font-semibold transition-all active:scale-95",
                  activeTab === "preview"
                    ? "bg-surface text-ink shadow-xs"
                    : "text-muted hover:text-ink"
                )}
              >
                <Eye className="size-3.5" />
                <span>Visualizar</span>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveTab("code")}
                className={cn(
                  "flex min-h-9 md:min-h-7 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-3 md:px-2.5 py-1.5 md:py-1 text-xs font-semibold transition-all active:scale-95",
                  activeTab === "code"
                    ? "bg-surface text-ink shadow-xs"
                    : "text-muted hover:text-ink"
                )}
              >
                <Code2 className="size-3.5" />
                <span>Código</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {activeTab === "code" && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleCopy}
                className="flex min-h-9 md:min-h-7 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface px-3 md:px-2.5 py-1.5 md:py-1 text-xs font-medium text-muted transition-all hover:border-primary hover:text-primary active:scale-95"
              >
                {copied ? (
                  <>
                    <Check className="size-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
                    <span className="text-emerald-600 font-semibold">Copiado!</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Copy className="size-3.5" />
                    <span>Copiar código</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="mt-4 flex h-[380px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line/60 bg-surface-raised/40 p-4">
          {activeTab === "code" && code ? (
            <pre className="h-full w-full overflow-auto rounded-lg border border-line/40 bg-[#091311] p-4 text-left text-xs font-mono leading-relaxed text-[#e6f4ef] selection:bg-primary/40 [scrollbar-width:thin]">
              <code>{code}</code>
            </pre>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center">
              {children ?? (
                <span className="text-xs font-medium text-muted">
                  {placeholder ?? "Em breve..."}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </article>
    </Reveal>
  );
}


================================================================================

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  Bookmark,
  Check,
  Compass,
  Home,
  Info,
  Plus,
  User,
} from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  Badge,
  BottomNav,
  BottomNavItem,
  BottomNavFab,
  SegmentedControl,
  SegmentedControlItem,
  type BottomNavVariant,
} from "@/components/ui";

const VARIATIONS_INFO: Record<
  BottomNavVariant,
  { label: string; tag: string; ergonomicNote: string; badgeColor: string }
> = {
  standard: {
    label: "Padrão (4 Destinos)",
    tag: "Zona do Polegar",
    ergonomicNote: "Posicionada na 'Natural Thumb Zone' inferior. Distribui 4 alvos simétricos de 48dp de largura por 56dp de altura, evitando toques falsos.",
    badgeColor: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-500/30",
  },
  "with-fab": {
    label: "Ação Central (FAB)",
    tag: "Ação Primária Fácil",
    ergonomicNote: "Gatilho central em relevo para a ação principal do app (ex: Criar/Novo), centralizado no arco de rotação confortável do polegar.",
    badgeColor: "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600 border-blue-500/30",
  },
  floating: {
    label: "Barra Flutuante (Pill)",
    tag: "Respiro de Borda",
    ergonomicNote: "Barra suspensa com cantos curvos e recuo das bordas físicas da tela, prevenindo acionamento acidental dos gestos de navegação do sistema operacional.",
    badgeColor: "bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-600 border-purple-500/30",
  },
};

export const BOTTOM_NAV_CODE = `// Exemplo com Tailwind CSS Puro (Zero Dependências)
// Ergonomia: Alvos táteis >= 48x48dp, feedback ativo imediato e posicionamento na Thumb Zone

import { useState } from "react";
import { Home, Compass, Bookmark, User, Plus } from "lucide-react";

export function BottomNavErgonomica() {
  const [abaAtiva, setAbaAtiva] = useState("inicio");

  const destinos = [
    { id: "inicio", label: "Início", icone: Home },
    { id: "explorar", label: "Explorar", icone: Compass },
    { id: "salvos", label: "Salvos", icone: Bookmark, badge: 3 },
    { id: "perfil", label: "Perfil", icone: User },
  ];

  return (
    <nav
      role="navigation"
      aria-label="Navegação principal"
      className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-50 flex h-16 w-full items-center justify-around border-t border-line bg-surface/95 px-2 pb-safe backdrop-blur-md"
    >
      {destinos.map((item) => {
        const ativo = abaAtiva === item.id;
        const Icone = item.icone;

        return (
          <button
            key={item.id}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setAbaAtiva(item.id)}
            aria-current={ativo ? "page" : undefined}
            className="group relative flex min-h-[48px] min-w-[48px] flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-xl py-1 text-xs transition-transform active:scale-95"
          >
            {/* Ícone com indicador de badge e cor dinâmica */}
            <div className="relative">
              <Icone
                className={\`size-5 transition-colors \${
                  ativo ? "text-primary" : "text-muted group-hover:text-ink"
                }\`}
              />
              {item.badge && (
                <span className="absolute -right-2 -top-1 flex size-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary text-[9px] font-bold text-on-primary">
                  {item.badge}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Rótulo de texto ergonômico */}
            <span
              className={\`text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors \${
                ativo ? "font-semibold text-primary" : "text-muted"
              }\`}
            >
              {item.label}
            </span>

            {/* Indicador sutil de página ativa */}
            {ativo && (
              <span className="absolute -top-[1px] h-0.5 w-6 rounded-full bg-primary" />
            )}
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </nav>
  );
}`;

type NavItem = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  icon: typeof Home;
  badge?: number;
};

const STANDARD_NAV_ITEMS: NavItem[] = [
  { id: "home", label: "Início", icon: Home },
  { id: "explore", label: "Explorar", icon: Compass },
  { id: "saved", label: "Salvos", icon: Bookmark, badge: 3 },
  { id: "profile", label: "Perfil", icon: User },
];

export function BottomNavShowcase() {
  const [variant, setVariant] = useState<BottomNavVariant>("standard");
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("home");
  const [lastAction, setLastAction] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const currentInfo = VARIATIONS_INFO[variant];

  const triggerNav = (label: string, id: string) => {
    setActiveTab(id);
    setLastAction(`Navegou para: ${label}`);
  };

  const triggerFab = () => {
    setLastAction("Ação Central (FAB) acionada!");
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-between gap-4 py-1">
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
          Variação do Organismo
        </span>
        <SegmentedControl
          value={variant}
          onValueChange={(val) => {
            const v = val as BottomNavVariant;
            setVariant(v);
            setLastAction(`Variação selecionada: ${VARIATIONS_INFO[v].label}`);
          }}
        >
          {(["standard", "with-fab", "floating"] as BottomNavVariant[]).map((v) => (
            <SegmentedControlItem key={v} value={v}>
              {VARIATIONS_INFO[v].label}
            </SegmentedControlItem>
          ))}
        </SegmentedControl>
      </div>

      <div className="relative flex h-[142px] w-full max-w-sm flex-col justify-end overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface shadow-xs">
        <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center p-3 text-center">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-ink">
            <span className="size-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 animate-pulse" />
            <span>
              {activeTab === "home" && "Tela: Início"}
              {activeTab === "explore" && "Tela: Explorar"}
              {activeTab === "saved" && "Tela: Salvos (3 itens)"}
              {activeTab === "profile" && "Tela: Perfil"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[10px] text-muted">
            Toque nos destinos abaixo para testar o feedback tátil
          </p>
        </div>

        {variant === "standard" && (
          <BottomNav variant="standard" aria-label="Navegação inferior padrão">
            {STANDARD_NAV_ITEMS.map((item) => (
              <BottomNavItem
                key={item.id}
                icon={item.icon}
                label={item.label}
                badge={item.badge}
                active={activeTab === item.id}
                onClick={() => triggerNav(item.label, item.id)}
              />
            ))}
          </BottomNav>
        )}

        {variant === "with-fab" && (
          <BottomNav variant="with-fab" aria-label="Navegação inferior com FAB">
            <BottomNavItem
              icon={Home}
              label="Início"
              active={activeTab === "home"}
              onClick={() => triggerNav("Início", "home")}
            />
            <BottomNavItem
              icon={Compass}
              label="Explorar"
              active={activeTab === "explore"}
              onClick={() => triggerNav("Explorar", "explore")}
            />
            <BottomNavFab
              icon={Plus}
              label="Criar"
              onClick={triggerFab}
              aria-label="Adicionar novo item"
            />
            <BottomNavItem
              icon={Bookmark}
              label="Salvos"
              badge={3}
              active={activeTab === "saved"}
              onClick={() => triggerNav("Salvos", "saved")}
            />
            <BottomNavItem
              icon={User}
              label="Perfil"
              active={activeTab === "profile"}
              onClick={() => triggerNav("Perfil", "profile")}
            />
          </BottomNav>
        )}

        {variant === "floating" && (
          <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-center pb-2.5 px-3">
            <BottomNav variant="floating" aria-label="Navegação inferior flutuante">
              {STANDARD_NAV_ITEMS.map((item) => (
                <BottomNavItem
                  key={item.id}
                  icon={item.icon}
                  title={item.label}
                  active={activeTab === item.id}
                  onClick={() => triggerNav(item.label, item.id)}
                />
              ))}
            </BottomNav>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="h-[86px] w-full shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line/60 bg-surface/70 p-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 whitespace-nowrap">
          <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-ink">
            <Info className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-primary" />
            <span className="truncate">Ergonomia ({currentInfo.label})</span>
          </span>
          <Badge className={cn("shrink-0", currentInfo.badgeColor)}>
            {currentInfo.tag}
          </Badge>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1.5 line-clamp-2 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">
          {currentInfo.ergonomicNote}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex h-[26px] w-full shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-2 whitespace-nowrap border-t border-line/50 pt-2 text-[11px] text-muted">
        <span className="truncate font-mono text-[10px]">
          Alvo: 48x48dp • Zona: Thumb Zone Inferior
        </span>
        <span className="flex min-w-0 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 font-medium">
          {lastAction ? (
            <>
              <Check className="size-3 shrink-0 text-emerald-600" />
              <span className="truncate font-semibold text-emerald-600">{lastAction}</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <span className="truncate text-muted">Toque em qualquer destino para testar</span>
          )}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/catalog/showcases/buttons-showcase.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Plus, Sparkles, Check, Info } from "lucide-react";
import {
  Badge,
  Button,
  FAB,
  SegmentedControl,
  SegmentedControlItem,
} from "@/components/ui";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

type ButtonSize = "sm" | "md" | "lg";

const SIZES_INFO: Record<
  ButtonSize,
  { label: string; height: string; tag: string; ergonomicNote: string; badgeColor: string }
> = {
  sm: {
    label: "P",
    height: "40px",
    tag: "Secundário / Inline",
    ergonomicNote: "Exige respiro lateral. Abaixo dos 48dp, use apenas para ações de apoio.",
    badgeColor: "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 border-amber-500/30",
  },
  md: {
    label: "M",
    height: "44px",
    tag: "Padrão Apple (iOS HIG)",
    ergonomicNote: "Alvo mínimo de 44pt da Apple. Excelente densidade para listas e cards.",
    badgeColor: "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600 border-blue-500/30",
  },
  lg: {
    label: "G",
    height: "48px",
    tag: "Padrão Ouro (Regra dos 48dp)",
    ergonomicNote: "Ideal para o polegar e conformidade total com a Regra dos 48dp e WCAG AAA.",
    badgeColor: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-500/30",
  },
};

export const BUTTONS_CODE = `// Exemplo com Hapto Design System
import { Plus, Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, FAB } from "@/components/ui";

export function BotoesErgonomicos() {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
      {/* 1. Botão Primário — 48px de altura (Regra dos 48dp para o polegar) */}
      <Button variant="primary" size="lg">
        <Sparkles className="size-4" />
        <span>Principal</span>
      </Button>

      {/* 2. Botão Secundário — 44px de altura (Padrão Apple HIG) */}
      <Button variant="secondary" size="md">
        <span>Secundário</span>
      </Button>

      {/* 3. FAB Flutuante — 56px de diâmetro (Alcance imediato do polegar) */}
      <FAB
        size="lg"
        label="Adicionar"
        icon={Plus}
      />
    </div>
  );
};`;

export function ButtonsShowcase() {
  const [size, setSize] = useState<ButtonSize>("lg");
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleClick = (name: string) => {
    setFeedback(`Toque: ${name}`);
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setFeedback(null), 1800);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  };

  const currentSizeInfo = SIZES_INFO[size];

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center gap-5 py-1">
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
          Calibrar Tamanho do Alvo Tátil
        </span>
        <SegmentedControl
          value={size}
          onValueChange={(val) => setSize(val as ButtonSize)}
        >
          {(["sm", "md", "lg"] as ButtonSize[]).map((s) => (
            <SegmentedControlItem
              key={s}
              value={s}
              detail={`(${SIZES_INFO[s].height})`}
            >
              {SIZES_INFO[s].label}
            </SegmentedControlItem>
          ))}
        </SegmentedControl>
      </div>

      <div className="flex w-full flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-3 py-2">
        <Button
          variant="primary"
          size={size}
          onClick={() => handleClick(`Principal (${currentSizeInfo.height})`)}
          className="shadow-sm hover:shadow-primary/20 hover:shadow-lg"
        >
          <Sparkles className="size-4" />
          <span>Principal</span>
        </Button>

        <Button
          variant="secondary"
          size={size}
          onClick={() => handleClick(`Secundário (${currentSizeInfo.height})`)}
        >
          <span>Secundário</span>
        </Button>

        <FAB
          size="lg"
          onClick={() => handleClick("FAB Flutuante (56px)")}
          label="Adicionar"
          icon={Plus}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="w-full rounded-xl border border-line/60 bg-surface/70 p-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-ink">
            <Info className="size-3.5 text-primary" />
            <span>Tamanho {currentSizeInfo.label} ({currentSizeInfo.height})</span>
          </span>
          <Badge className={cn("shrink-0", currentSizeInfo.badgeColor)}>
            {currentSizeInfo.tag}
          </Badge>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">
          {currentSizeInfo.ergonomicNote}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-between border-t border-line/50 pt-2 text-[11px] text-muted">
        <span className="font-mono text-[10px]">Alvo ativo: {currentSizeInfo.height}</span>
        <span className="font-medium text-ink">
          {feedback ? (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-primary animate-in fade-in">
              <Check className="size-3" />
              {feedback}
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-muted/60">Experimente tocar nos botões</span>
          )}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

ARQUIVO: components/catalog/showcases/cards-showcase.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

    tag: "Escaneabilidade",
    ergonomicNote: "Número em destaque para leitura rápida em telas pequenas, com zona de avanço tátil clara.",
    badgeColor: "bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-600 border-purple-500/30",
  },
};

export const CARDS_CODE = `// Exemplo com Hapto Design System
import { useState } from "react";
import { ChevronRight, Bookmark, Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import { Card, CardTitle, CardDescription, CardAction, CardFooter, Badge } from "@/components/ui";

export function CardInformativoErgonomico() {
  const [salvo, setSalvo] = useState(false);

  return (
    <Card
      as="article"
      variant="interactive"
      onClick={() => alert("Card acionado!")}
      className="w-full max-w-sm"
    >
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <Badge variant="primary" shape="pill" icon={<Sparkles className="size-3" />}>
          Destaque
        </Badge>

        {/* Ação secundária isolada (CardAction com stopPropagation automático) */}
        <CardAction
          aria-label="Salvar nos favoritos"
          onClick={() => setSalvo(!salvo)}
        >
          <Bookmark className={\`size-5 \${salvo ? "fill-primary text-primary" : "text-muted"}\`} />
        </CardAction>
      </div>

      <CardTitle className="mt-3">Design System Háptico</CardTitle>
      <CardDescription className="mt-1">
        Padrões ergonômicos de toque e feedback tátil para aplicações mobile-first.
      </CardDescription>

      <CardFooter className="mt-4">
        <span>Explorar módulo</span>
        <ChevronRight className="size-4" />
      </CardFooter>
    </Card>
  );
};`;

export function CardsShowcase() {
  const [variant, setVariant] = useState<CardVariant>("interactive");
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const [lastAction, setLastAction] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const timerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  const currentInfo = VARIATIONS_INFO[variant];

  useEffect(() => () => {
    if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
  }, []);

  const triggerAction = (label: string) => {
    setLastAction(label);
    if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
    timerRef.current = setTimeout(() => setLastAction(null), 1800);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full w-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2">
      <SegmentedControl
        value={variant}
        onValueChange={(val) => setVariant(val as CardVariant)}
      >
        {(["interactive", "with-action", "metric"] as CardVariant[]).map((v) => (
          <SegmentedControlItem key={v} value={v}>
            {VARIATIONS_INFO[v].label}
          </SegmentedControlItem>
        ))}
      </SegmentedControl>

      <div className="h-[160px] w-full max-w-sm shrink-0">
        {variant === "interactive" && (
          <Card
            variant="interactive"
            onClick={() => triggerAction("Card aberto (Superfície Total)")}
            className="flex h-full w-full flex-col justify-between"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 whitespace-nowrap">
              <Badge
                variant="neutral"
                shape="pill"
                icon={<Smartphone className="size-3 text-primary" />}
              >
                Mobile First
              </Badge>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] text-muted">
                <Clock className="size-3" />
                <span>3 min de leitura</span>
              </span>
            </div>

            <div>
              <CardTitle className="truncate text-sm">
                Regra dos 48dp no Polegar
              </CardTitle>
              <CardDescription className="mt-0.5 line-clamp-2">
                Alvos de toque calibrados para prevenir falhas e fadiga muscular em smartphones.
              </CardDescription>
            </div>

            <CardFooter>
              <span>Acessar conteúdo</span>
              <ChevronRight className="size-4 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1" />
            </CardFooter>
          </Card>
        )}

        {variant === "with-action" && (
          <Card
            variant="interactive"
            onClick={() => triggerAction("Card principal acionado")}
            className="flex h-full w-full flex-col justify-between"
          >
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
              <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2.5">
                <div className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-primary-light text-primary shadow-2xs">
                  <Sparkles className="size-4.5" />
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <CardTitle className="truncate text-sm">
                    Biblioteca de Gestos
                  </CardTitle>
                  <span className="block truncate text-[10px] text-muted">24 componentes táteis</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <CardAction
                aria-label="Salvar card nos favoritos"
                onClick={() => {
                  setSaved(!saved);
                  triggerAction(saved ? "Removido dos favoritos" : "Salvo nos favoritos!");
                }}
                className={cn(
                  "border text-muted",
                  saved
                    ? "border-primary/40 bg-primary/10 text-primary shadow-xs"
                    : "border-line bg-surface hover:border-primary/50 hover:text-ink"
                )}
                title={saved ? "Salvo" : "Salvar"}
              >
                <Bookmark
                  className={cn(
                    "size-4.5 transition-transform duration-200",
                    saved && "fill-primary scale-110"
                  )}
                />
              </CardAction>
            </div>

            <CardDescription className="line-clamp-2">
              Toque no ícone à direita para isolar a ação sem abrir o card principal.
            </CardDescription>

            <CardFooter>
              <span>Abrir biblioteca</span>
              <ChevronRight className="size-4 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1" />
            </CardFooter>
          </Card>
        )}

        {variant === "metric" && (
          <Card
            variant="interactive"
            onClick={() => triggerAction("Relatório de ergonomia aberto")}
            className="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-between"
          >
            <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3.5">
              <div className="flex size-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600">
                <TrendingUp className="size-5.5" />
              </div>
              <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col">
                <span className="truncate text-[11px] font-semibold text-muted">Acessibilidade Tátil</span>
                <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-ink">98.4%</span>
                <span className="truncate text-[10px] font-medium text-emerald-600">
                  +12.8% vs. alvos de 32px
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface-raised text-muted transition-colors group-hover:border-primary group-hover:text-primary">
              <ArrowRight className="size-4" />
            </div>
          </Card>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="h-[86px] w-full shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line/60 bg-surface/70 p-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 whitespace-nowrap">
          <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-ink">
            <Info className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-primary" />
            <span className="truncate">Ergonomia ({currentInfo.label})</span>
          </span>
          <Badge className={cn("shrink-0", currentInfo.badgeColor)}>
            {currentInfo.tag}
          </Badge>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1.5 line-clamp-2 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">
          {currentInfo.ergonomicNote}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex h-[26px] w-full shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-2 whitespace-nowrap border-t border-line/50 pt-2 text-[11px] text-muted">
        <span className="truncate font-mono text-[10px]">
          Alvo: Superfície Total • Respiro: 16px
        </span>
        <span className="flex min-w-0 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 font-medium">
          {lastAction ? (
            <>
              <Check className="size-3 shrink-0 text-emerald-600" />
              <span className="truncate font-semibold text-emerald-600">{lastAction}</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <span className="truncate text-muted">Toque no card para testar</span>
          )}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

ARQUIVO: components/catalog/showcases/colors-showcase.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  Info,
  Sun,
  XCircle,
} from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

type ColorsVariant = "contrast-checker" | "semantic-palette" | "sunlight-sim";

const VARIATIONS_INFO: Record<
  ColorsVariant,
  { label: string; tag: string; ergonomicNote: string; badgeColor: string }
> = {
  "contrast-checker": {
    label: "WCAG 2.1 (4.5:1 / 3:1)",
    tag: "Acessibilidade AA",
    ergonomicNote: "Exige contraste mínimo de 4.5:1 para textos padrão e 3:1 para textos grandes (≥18pt ou 14pt bold), assegurando legibilidade para pessoas com baixa acuidade visual.",
    badgeColor: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-500/30",
  },
  "semantic-palette": {
    label: "Paleta Semântica",
    tag: "Tokens de Cor",
    ergonomicNote: "Cores mapeadas por função: Ink (texto principal), Muted (secundário acessível), Primary (gatilhos táteis) e Surface (planos de repouso dos dedos).",
    badgeColor: "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600 border-blue-500/30",
  },
  "sunlight-sim": {
    label: "Simulador Luz Solar",
    tag: "Uso Externo",
    ergonomicNote: "O brilho solar e os reflexos no vidro da tela degradam o contraste percebido em até 50%. Cores com 4.5:1 sobrevivem, enquanto contrastes fracos somem na rua.",
    badgeColor: "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 border-amber-500/30",
  },
};

export const COLORS_CODE = `// Tokens de Cores Ergonômicas com Contraste WCAG 2.1 AA (Zero Dependências)
// Regra: Texto normal >= 4.5:1 | Texto grande >= 3.0:1 | Componentes >= 3.0:1

:root {
  /* Fundo & Superfícies */
  --bg: #f9fbf9;
  --surface: #ffffff;
  --surface-raised: #f0f4f1;
  --line: #e1e8e3;

  /* Textos com Contraste Calibrado */
  --ink: #111a14;        /* Contraste 14.2:1 vs Fundo (Passa WCAG AAA) */
  --muted: #526357;      /* Contraste 5.1:1 vs Fundo (Passa WCAG AA >= 4.5:1) */

  /* Cores de Ação & Interação Tátil */
  --primary: #128a58;    /* Contraste 4.6:1 vs Fundo Branco */
  --on-primary: #ffffff; /* Contraste 4.6:1 vs Primary */
  --primary-light: #e6f6ee;
}

/* Modo Escuro Ergonômico (Evita contraste puro 21:1 que gera fadiga/astigmatismo) */
.dark {
  --bg: #0b110e;
  --surface: #121a15;
  --surface-raised: #1a251f;
  --line: #24332b;
  --ink: #f2f7f4;        /* Contraste 13.8:1 */
  --muted: #94a89c;      /* Contraste 5.4:1 */
  --primary: #22c57a;    /* Contraste 7.1:1 no Dark Mode */
  --on-primary: #05140b;
}`;

type ContrastPair = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  fg: string;
  bg: string;
  ratio: number;
  type: "normal" | "large" | "fail";
  description: string;
};

const CONTRAST_PAIRS: ContrastPair[] = [
  {
    id: "ink-surface",
    name: "Ink no Surface",
    fg: "text-ink",
    bg: "bg-surface",
    ratio: 14.2,
    type: "normal",
    description: "Texto padrão (14-16px): 14.2:1",
  },
  {
    id: "muted-surface",
    name: "Muted no Surface",
    fg: "text-muted",
    bg: "bg-surface",
    ratio: 5.1,
    type: "normal",
    description: "Secundário acessível: 5.1:1",
  },
  {
    id: "primary-white",
    name: "Primary no Surface",
    fg: "text-primary",
    bg: "bg-surface",
    ratio: 4.6,
    type: "normal",
    description: "Ação de toque: 4.6:1",
  },
  {
    id: "fail-example",
    name: "Cinza Claro (Inválido)",
    fg: "text-line",
    bg: "bg-surface",
    ratio: 2.1,
    type: "fail",
    description: "Inadequado: 2.1:1 (Reprovado)",
  },
];

export function ColorsShowcase() {
  const [variant, setVariant] = useState<ColorsVariant>("contrast-checker");
  const [selectedPair, setSelectedPair] = useState<string>("ink-surface");
  const [sunlightActive, setSunlightActive] = useState(false);
  const [lastAction, setLastAction] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const currentInfo = VARIATIONS_INFO[variant];
  const activePair = CONTRAST_PAIRS.find((p) => p.id === selectedPair) || CONTRAST_PAIRS[0];

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-between gap-4 py-1">
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
          Variação do Token de Cor
        </span>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface p-1 shadow-xs">
          {(["contrast-checker", "semantic-palette", "sunlight-sim"] as ColorsVariant[]).map((v) => {
            const isSelected = variant === v;
            const item = VARIATIONS_INFO[v];
            return (
              <button
                key={v}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setVariant(v);
                  setLastAction(`Variação: ${item.label}`);
                }}
                aria-pressed={isSelected}
                className={cn(
                  "flex min-h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-3 text-xs font-semibold transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary",
                  isSelected
                    ? "bg-primary text-on-primary shadow-xs"
                    : "text-muted hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink"
                )}
              >
                <span>{item.label}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="relative flex h-[142px] w-full max-w-sm flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-3 shadow-xs">
        {variant === "contrast-checker" && (
          <div className="flex h-full flex-col justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-muted">Amostra:</span>
              <div className="flex gap-1">
                {CONTRAST_PAIRS.map((pair) => (
                  <button
                    key={pair.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setSelectedPair(pair.id);
                      setLastAction(`Contraste: ${pair.name} (${pair.ratio}:1)`);
                    }}
                    className={cn(
                      "rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold transition-all active:scale-95",
                      selectedPair === pair.id
                        ? "border-primary bg-primary/10 text-primary"
                        : "border-line bg-surface text-muted hover:text-ink"
                    )}
                  >
                    {pair.ratio}:1
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface-raised/70 p-2.5">
              <div className="flex flex-col">
                <span className={cn("text-xs font-bold", activePair.fg)}>
                  {activePair.name}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">
                  {activePair.ratio >= 4.5
                    ? "Legível em texto padrão (14-16px)"
                    : activePair.ratio >= 3.0
                    ? "Legível apenas em texto grande (≥18px)"
                    : "Ilegível sob luz solar ou baixa visão"}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="font-mono text-sm font-extrabold text-ink">
                  {activePair.ratio}:1
                </span>
                {activePair.ratio >= 4.5 ? (
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold text-emerald-600">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="size-3" />
                    <span>AA Pass</span>
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded-full bg-red-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold text-red-500">
                    <XCircle className="size-3" />
                    <span>Fail</span>
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-muted">
              <span>Mínimo Texto Normal: <strong>4.5:1</strong></span>
              <span>Mínimo Texto Grande: <strong>3.0:1</strong></span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {variant === "semantic-palette" && (
          <div className="flex h-full flex-col justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-ink">Tokens de Superfície e Contraste</span>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">Todos &ge; 4.5:1</span>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2">
              <div
                onClick={() => setLastAction("Token: Primary (#128a58) • 4.6:1")}
                className="group flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface-raised p-1.5 transition-all hover:border-primary active:scale-95"
              >
                <div className="size-5 rounded-full bg-primary shadow-xs" />
                <span className="mt-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-ink">Primary</span>
                <span className="text-[9px] font-medium text-emerald-600">4.6:1</span>
              </div>

              <div
                onClick={() => setLastAction("Token: Ink (#111a14) • 14.2:1")}
                className="group flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface-raised p-1.5 transition-all hover:border-primary active:scale-95"
              >
                <div className="size-5 rounded-full bg-ink shadow-xs" />
                <span className="mt-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-ink">Ink</span>
                <span className="text-[9px] font-medium text-emerald-600">14.2:1</span>
              </div>

              <div
                onClick={() => setLastAction("Token: Muted (#526357) • 5.1:1")}
                className="group flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface-raised p-1.5 transition-all hover:border-primary active:scale-95"
              >
                <div className="size-5 rounded-full bg-muted shadow-xs" />
                <span className="mt-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-ink">Muted</span>
                <span className="text-[9px] font-medium text-emerald-600">5.1:1</span>
              </div>

              <div
                onClick={() => setLastAction("Token: Surface (#ffffff) • Base tátil")}
                className="group flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface-raised p-1.5 transition-all hover:border-primary active:scale-95"
              >
                <div className="size-5 rounded-full border border-line bg-surface shadow-xs" />
                <span className="mt-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-ink">Surface</span>
                <span className="text-[9px] font-medium text-muted">Base</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <p className="truncate text-center text-[10px] text-muted">
              Toque nos tokens para ver o ratio e a função ergonômica

          </div>
        )}

        {variant === "sunlight-sim" && (
          <div className="flex h-full flex-col justify-between">

                <span>Simulador de Luz Solar Externa</span>
              </div>

              <button

                  setLastAction(sunlightActive ? "Ambiente interno restaurado" : "Simulação solar ativada (-50% contraste)");
                }}
                className={cn(
                  "rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold transition-all active:scale-95",
                  sunlightActive
                    ? "border-amber-500 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600"
                    : "border-line bg-surface text-muted"
                )}
              >
                {sunlightActive ? "☀️ Sol Ativo" : "🏠 Interno"}
              </button>
            </div>

            <div
              className={cn(
                "relative flex items-center justify-around rounded-xl border border-line p-2 transition-all",
                sunlightActive ? "bg-amber-100/40 opacity-70 contrast-75 brightness-110" : "bg-surface-raised"
              )}
            >
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink">Hapto (4.5:1+)</span>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink">Texto Legível</span>
              </div>

              <div className="h-6 w-px bg-line" />

              <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
                <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">Baixo Contraste (2.0:1)</span>
                <span className={cn("text-xs font-medium", sunlightActive ? "opacity-20 blur-[0.5px]" : "text-muted/60")}>
                  {sunlightActive ? "(Ilegível)" : "Texto Apagado"}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <p className="truncate text-center text-[10px] text-muted">
              {sunlightActive
                ? "Sob luz solar, o contraste percebido cai pela metade."
                : "Ative o simulador solar para ver o impacto na legibilidade."}
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="h-[86px] w-full shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line/60 bg-surface/70 p-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 whitespace-nowrap">
          <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-ink">
            <Info className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-primary" />
            <span className="truncate">Ergonomia ({currentInfo.label})</span>
          </span>
          <span
            className={cn(
              "shrink-0 rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider",
              currentInfo.badgeColor
            )}
          >
            {currentInfo.tag}
          </span>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1.5 line-clamp-2 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">
          {currentInfo.ergonomicNote}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex h-[26px] w-full shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-2 whitespace-nowrap border-t border-line/50 pt-2 text-[11px] text-muted">
        <span className="truncate font-mono text-[10px]">
          WCAG 2.1 AA • Normal: 4.5:1 • Grande: 3:1
        </span>
        <span className="flex min-w-0 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 font-medium">
          {lastAction ? (
            <>
              <Check className="size-3 shrink-0 text-emerald-600" />
              <span className="truncate font-semibold text-emerald-600">{lastAction}</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <span className="truncate text-muted">Interaja com os tokens de cor</span>
          )}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

ARQUIVO: components/catalog/showcases/feedback-showcase.tsx
