"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  AlertTriangle,
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  Info,
  RefreshCw,
  RotateCcw,
  Sparkles,
} from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  Badge,
  Feedback,
  FeedbackIcon,
  FeedbackTitle,
  FeedbackDescription,
  FeedbackActions,
  FeedbackAction,
  FeedbackClose,
  SegmentedControl,
  SegmentedControlItem,
} from "@/components/ui";

type FeedbackVariant = "snackbar" | "banner" | "floating";

const VARIATIONS_INFO: Record<
  FeedbackVariant,
  { label: string; tag: string; ergonomicNote: string; badgeColor: string }
> = {
  snackbar: {
    label: "Snackbar Inferior",
    tag: "Ação na Thumb Zone",
    ergonomicNote: "Posicionada na base da tela para permitir que o polegar acione 'Desfazer' ou 'Ver' instantaneamente sem reajustar a pegada do smartphone.",
    badgeColor: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-500/30",
  },
  banner: {
    label: "Alerta em Card",
    tag: "Contexto Visual",
    ergonomicNote: "Card de aviso estruturado com contraste tátil e botão de ação compacto, ideal para informações persistentes que demandam atenção.",
    badgeColor: "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600 border-blue-500/30",
  },
  floating: {
    label: "Toast Flutuante (Pill)",
    tag: "Dispensar Rápido",
    ergonomicNote: "Pílula de notificação flutuante com feedback de sucesso/erro de leitura rápida, botão de fechar com hitbox generosa e auto-dispensa.",
    badgeColor: "bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-600 border-purple-500/30",
  },
};

export const FEEDBACK_CODE = `// Exemplo com componente modular Feedback (Hapto Design System)
// Ergonomia: Ancorado na base da tela (Thumb Zone) para alcance imediato do botão "Desfazer"

import { useState } from "react";
import { CheckCircle2, RotateCcw } from "lucide-react";
import {
  Feedback,
  FeedbackIcon,
  FeedbackTitle,
  FeedbackActions,
  FeedbackAction,
  FeedbackClose,
} from "@/components/ui";

export function SnackbarErgonomica() {
  const [visivel, setVisivel] = useState(true);

  if (!visivel) return null;

  return (
    <Feedback variant="snackbar" fixed>
      {/* 1. Ícone + Mensagem Contextual */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <FeedbackIcon icon={CheckCircle2} className="text-emerald-500" />
        <FeedbackTitle>Item arquivado com sucesso</FeedbackTitle>
      </div>

      {/* 2. Botão de Ação Imediata (Desfazer) na Zona de Alcance */}
      <FeedbackActions>
        <FeedbackAction
          icon={RotateCcw}
          onClick={() => {
            alert("Ação desfeita!");
            setVisivel(false);
          }}
        >
          Desfazer
        </FeedbackAction>
        <FeedbackClose onClick={() => setVisivel(false)} />
      </FeedbackActions>
    </Feedback>
  );
};`;

export function FeedbackShowcase() {
  const [variant, setVariant] = useState<FeedbackVariant>("snackbar");
  const [activeNotification, setActiveNotification] = useState(true);
  const [lastAction, setLastAction] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const currentInfo = VARIATIONS_INFO[variant];



  const triggerUndo = () => {
    setActiveNotification(false);
    setLastAction("Ação revertida (Desfazer)!");
  };

  const triggerDismiss = () => {
    setActiveNotification(false);
    setLastAction("Notificação dispensada.");
  };

  const triggerAction = (msg: string) => {
    setLastAction(msg);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-between gap-4 py-1">
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
          Variação do Organismo
        </span>
        <SegmentedControl
          value={variant}
          onValueChange={(val) => {
            const v = val as FeedbackVariant;
            setVariant(v);
            setActiveNotification(true);
            setLastAction(`Variação selecionada: ${VARIATIONS_INFO[v].label}`);
          }}
        >
          {(["snackbar", "banner", "floating"] as FeedbackVariant[]).map((v) => (
            <SegmentedControlItem key={v} value={v}>
              {VARIATIONS_INFO[v].label}
            </SegmentedControlItem>
          ))}
        </SegmentedControl>
      </div>

      <div className="relative flex h-[142px] w-full max-w-sm flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-3 shadow-xs">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-ink">
              <span className="size-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 animate-pulse" />
              <span>Tela Mobile Ativa</span>
            </div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">Zona de Feedback</span>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1 opacity-25">
            <div className="h-1.5 w-3/5 rounded-full bg-ink" />
            <div className="h-1.5 w-2/5 rounded-full bg-ink" />
          </div>
        </div>

        {activeNotification ? (
          <div>
            {variant === "snackbar" && (
              <Feedback variant="snackbar">
                <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
                  <FeedbackIcon icon={CheckCircle2} className="text-emerald-500" />
                  <FeedbackTitle>Item salvo nos favoritos</FeedbackTitle>
                </div>

                <FeedbackActions>
                  <FeedbackAction icon={RotateCcw} onClick={triggerUndo}>
                    Desfazer
                  </FeedbackAction>
                  <FeedbackClose aria-label="Dispensar" onClick={triggerDismiss} />
                </FeedbackActions>
              </Feedback>
            )}

            {variant === "banner" && (
              <Feedback variant="banner" status="warning">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                  <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5">
                    <FeedbackIcon icon={AlertTriangle} />
                    <FeedbackTitle>Modo offline ativado</FeedbackTitle>
                  </div>
                  <FeedbackClose aria-label="Fechar alerta" onClick={triggerDismiss} />
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <FeedbackDescription>Sincronização em pausa</FeedbackDescription>
                  <FeedbackAction onClick={() => triggerAction("Tentando reconexão...")}>
                    Reconectar
                  </FeedbackAction>
                </div>
              </Feedback>
            )}

            {variant === "floating" && (
              <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-center">
                <Feedback variant="floating">
                  <FeedbackIcon icon={Sparkles} />
                  <FeedbackTitle>Preferências salvas</FeedbackTitle>
                  <FeedbackClose aria-label="Dispensar toast" onClick={triggerDismiss} />
                </Feedback>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-dashed border-line bg-surface-raised/40 px-3 py-2">
            <span className="text-[11px] text-muted">Notificação fechada</span>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setActiveNotification(true);
                setLastAction("Notificação reaberta!");
              }}
              className="flex min-h-7 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface px-2.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-primary transition-all hover:border-primary active:scale-95"
            >
              <RefreshCw className="size-3" />
              <span>Disparar novamente</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="h-[86px] w-full shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line/60 bg-surface/70 p-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 whitespace-nowrap">
          <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-ink">
            <Info className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-primary" />
            <span className="truncate">Ergonomia ({currentInfo.label})</span>
          </span>
          <Badge className={cn("shrink-0", currentInfo.badgeColor)}>
            {currentInfo.tag}
          </Badge>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1.5 line-clamp-2 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">
          {currentInfo.ergonomicNote}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex h-[26px] w-full shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-2 whitespace-nowrap border-t border-line/50 pt-2 text-[11px] text-muted">
        <span className="truncate font-mono text-[10px]">
          Posicionamento: Base • Alcance: Thumb Zone
        </span>
        <span className="flex min-w-0 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 font-medium">
          {lastAction ? (
            <>
              <Check className="size-3 shrink-0 text-emerald-600" />
              <span className="truncate font-semibold text-emerald-600">{lastAction}</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <span className="truncate text-muted">Toque nas ações para testar</span>
          )}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

ARQUIVO: components/catalog/showcases/icons-showcase.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  Bell,
  Bookmark,
  Check,
  Crosshair,
  Heart,
  Info,
  MoreHorizontal,
  Share2,
} from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  Badge,
  IconButton,
  SegmentedControl,
  SegmentedControlItem,
} from "@/components/ui";

type IconVariant = "action" | "badge" | "cluster";

const VARIATIONS_INFO: Record<
  IconVariant,
  { label: string; tag: string; ergonomicNote: string; badgeColor: string }
> = {
  action: {
    label: "Icon Button",
    tag: "Hitbox 48dp",
    ergonomicNote: "O glifo visual mede 20-24px, mas o alvo de toque cobre 48x48dp. O usuário aciona a ação sem necessidade de precisão milimétrica.",
    badgeColor: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-500/30",
  },
  badge: {
    label: "Com Notificação",
    tag: "Feedback de Estado",
    ergonomicNote: "Badge fixado no canto superior sem obstruir o reconhecimento da silhueta do ícone nem reduzir a área de clique de 48dp.",
    badgeColor: "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600 border-blue-500/30",
  },
  cluster: {
    label: "Grid de Ações",
    tag: "Respiro de 8dp",
    ergonomicNote: "Espaçamento mínimo de 8dp entre alvos de toque adjacentes para evitar toques acidentais no comando vizinho.",
    badgeColor: "bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-600 border-purple-500/30",
  },
};

export const ICONS_CODE = `// Exemplo com Hapto Design System
// Hitbox calibrada de 48x48dp com suporte a feedback e badges de estado

import { useState } from "react";
import { Bell, Bookmark, Heart, Share2 } from "lucide-react";
import { IconButton } from "@/components/ui";

export function IconesErgonomicos() {
  const [curtido, setCurtido] = useState(false);
  const [salvo, setSalvo] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
      {/* 1. Icon Button com Alvo Tátil de 48x48dp (Regra dos 48dp) */}
      <IconButton
        size="lg"
        aria-label="Curtir"
        onClick={() => setCurtido(!curtido)}
      >
        <Heart
          className={\`size-5 transition-transform duration-200 \${
            curtido ? "fill-red-500 text-red-500 scale-110" : "text-muted"
          }\`}
        />
      </IconButton>

      {/* 2. Ícone com Badge de Notificação Efêmero */}
      <IconButton
        size="lg"
        badge={true}
        aria-label="Notificações"
      >
        <Bell className="size-5 text-muted" />
      </IconButton>

      {/* 3. Ícone com Estado Salvo / Bookmark */}
      <IconButton
        size="lg"
        aria-label="Salvar item"
        onClick={() => setSalvo(!salvo)}
      >
        <Bookmark
          className={\`size-5 transition-transform duration-200 \${
            salvo ? "fill-primary text-primary scale-110" : "text-muted"
          }\`}
        />
      </IconButton>

      {/* 4. Ação com Respiro de Segurança de 8dp */}
      <IconButton
        size="lg"
        variant="ghost"
        aria-label="Compartilhar"
      >
        <Share2 className="size-5" />
      </IconButton>
    </div>
  );
};`;

export function IconsShowcase() {
  const [variant, setVariant] = useState<IconVariant>("action");
  const [showHitbox, setShowHitbox] = useState(true);

  const [liked, setLiked] = useState(false);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const [hasNotif, setHasNotif] = useState(true);
  const [toastMessage, setToastMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const showToast = (msg: string) => {
    setToastMessage(msg);
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setToastMessage(null), 1600);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  };

  const currentInfo = VARIATIONS_INFO[variant];

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-between gap-4 py-1">
      <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center gap-2">
        <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-between px-1">
          <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
            Variação Ergonômica
          </span>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setShowHitbox(!showHitbox)}
            className={cn(
              "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold transition-all active:scale-95",
              showHitbox
                ? "border-primary/40 bg-primary/10 text-primary"
                : "border-line bg-surface text-muted hover:text-ink"
            )}
            title="Alternar visualização da área tátil de 48dp"
          >
            <Crosshair className="size-3" />
            <span>Hitbox 48dp: {showHitbox ? "Visível" : "Oculta"}</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <SegmentedControl
          value={variant}
          onValueChange={(val) => setVariant(val as IconVariant)}
        >
          {(["action", "badge", "cluster"] as IconVariant[]).map((v) => (
            <SegmentedControlItem key={v} value={v}>
              {VARIATIONS_INFO[v].label}
            </SegmentedControlItem>
          ))}
        </SegmentedControl>
      </div>

      <div className="flex min-h-[96px] w-full max-w-sm items-center justify-center px-1">
        {variant === "action" && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="relative">
              {showHitbox && (
                <div
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  className="pointer-events-none absolute -inset-1 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-primary/50 bg-primary/5 animate-pulse"
                />
              )}
              <IconButton
                aria-label="Curtir"
                onClick={() => {
                  setLiked(!liked);
                  showToast(!liked ? "Curtido!" : "Descurtido");
                }}
                className={cn(
                  liked
                    ? "border-red-500/40 text-red-500 shadow-xs"
                    : "border-line text-muted hover:border-primary/50 hover:text-ink"
                )}
              >
                <Heart
                  className={cn(
                    "size-5 transition-transform duration-200",
                    liked && "fill-red-500 scale-110"
                  )}
                />
              </IconButton>
              {showHitbox && (
                <span className="absolute -bottom-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 text-[9px] font-mono font-bold text-primary">
                  48dp
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="relative">
              {showHitbox && (
                <div
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  className="pointer-events-none absolute -inset-1 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-primary/50 bg-primary/5 animate-pulse"
                />
              )}
              <IconButton
                aria-label="Salvar"
                onClick={() => {
                  setSaved(!saved);
                  showToast(!saved ? "Salvo nos favoritos!" : "Removido");
                }}
                className={cn(
                  saved
                    ? "border-primary/40 text-primary shadow-xs"
                    : "border-line text-muted hover:border-primary/50 hover:text-ink"
                )}
              >
                <Bookmark
                  className={cn(
                    "size-5 transition-transform duration-200",
                    saved && "fill-primary scale-110"
                  )}
                />
              </IconButton>
              {showHitbox && (
                <span className="absolute -bottom-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 text-[9px] font-mono font-bold text-primary">
                  48dp
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {variant === "badge" && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
            <div className="relative">
              {showHitbox && (
                <div
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  className="pointer-events-none absolute -inset-1 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-primary/50 bg-primary/5 animate-pulse"
                />
              )}
              <IconButton
                aria-label="Notificações"
                badge={hasNotif}
                onClick={() => {
                  setHasNotif(!hasNotif);
                  showToast(hasNotif ? "Notificações marcadas como lidas" : "Novas notificações!");
                }}
                className="text-ink"
              >
                <Bell className="size-5 text-ink" />
              </IconButton>
              {showHitbox && (
                <span className="absolute -bottom-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 text-[9px] font-mono font-bold text-primary">
                  48x48dp
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            <span className="text-xs text-muted">
              {hasNotif ? "1 notificação não lida (Toque p/ limpar)" : "Nenhuma notificação (Toque p/ gerar)"}
            </span>
          </div>
        )}

        {variant === "cluster" && (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-1.5 shadow-2xs">
              {[
                { icon: Heart, label: "Curtir", action: () => showToast("Curtido!") },
                { icon: Share2, label: "Compartilhar", action: () => showToast("Link copiado!") },
                { icon: Bookmark, label: "Salvar", action: () => showToast("Salvo!") },
                { icon: MoreHorizontal, label: "Mais ações", action: () => showToast("Menu aberto") },
              ].map((item, idx) => {
                const IconComponent = item.icon;
                return (
                  <div key={idx} className="relative">
                    {showHitbox && (
                      <div
                        aria-hidden="true"
                        className="pointer-events-none absolute -inset-0.5 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-primary/40 bg-primary/5"
                      />
                    )}
                    <IconButton
                      size="md"
                      variant="ghost"
                      aria-label={item.label}
                      onClick={item.action}
                      className="hover:text-primary"
                    >
                      <IconComponent className="size-4.5" />
                    </IconButton>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            {showHitbox && (
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-semibold text-primary">
                Gap de 8dp entre alvos de toque
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="w-full rounded-xl border border-line/60 bg-surface/70 p-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-ink">
            <Info className="size-3.5 text-primary" />
            <span>Ergonomia ({currentInfo.label})</span>
          </span>
          <Badge className={cn("shrink-0", currentInfo.badgeColor)}>
            {currentInfo.tag}
          </Badge>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">
          {currentInfo.ergonomicNote}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-between border-t border-line/50 pt-2 text-[11px] text-muted">
        <span className="font-mono text-[10px]">
          Alvo: {variant === "cluster" ? "44x44dp (M)" : "48x48dp (G)"} • Glifo: 20px
        </span>
        <span className="font-medium text-ink flex items-center gap-1">
          {toastMessage ? (
            <>
              <Check className="size-3 text-emerald-600" />
              <span className="text-emerald-600 font-semibold">{toastMessage}</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <span>Interativo: toque para testar</span>
          )}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Check, Info, Mail, X } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  Badge,
  Input,
  SegmentedControl,
  SegmentedControlItem,
} from "@/components/ui";

type InputState = "normal" | "icon" | "error";

const STATES_INFO: Record<
  InputState,
  { label: string; tag: string; ergonomicNote: string; badgeColor: string }
> = {
  normal: {
    label: "Padrão (48dp)",
    tag: "Essencial",
    ergonomicNote: "Altura de 48dp garante foco imediato no polegar. Fonte de 16px evita o auto-zoom do iOS.",
    badgeColor: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-500/30",
  },
  icon: {
    label: "Com Ícone",
    tag: "Apoio Visual",
    ergonomicNote: "Ícone interno com recuo seguro (pl-11) para não invadir a área de digitação legível.",
    badgeColor: "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600 border-blue-500/30",
  },
  error: {
    label: "Com Erro",
    tag: "Acessibilidade",
    ergonomicNote: "Borda com contraste alto e mensagem descritiva imediata sem depender apenas de cor.",
    badgeColor: "bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-600 border-rose-500/30",
  },
};

export const INPUTS_CODE = `// Exemplo com Hapto Design System
import { useState } from "react";
import { Mail, X } from "lucide-react";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui";

export function InputErgonomico() {
  const [valor, setValor] = useState("");

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-1.5">
      <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-ink">
        E-mail de Contato
      </label>

      {/* Input de 48dp (Prevenção de auto-zoom no iOS) */}
      <Input
        inputSize="lg"
        type="email"
        value={valor}
        onChange={(e) => setValor(e.target.value)}
        placeholder="exemplo@hapto.design"
        startIcon={<Mail className="size-5" />}
        endIcon={
          valor ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setValor("")}
              aria-label="Limpar campo"
              className="flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-muted hover:bg-surface-raised active:scale-90"
            >
              <X className="size-4" />
            </button>
          ) : undefined
        }
        helperText="Alvo tátil confortável de 48dp e sem zoom involuntário no mobile."
      />
    </div>
  );
};`;

export function InputsShowcase() {
  const [state, setState] = useState<InputState>("normal");
  const [value, setValue] = useState("contato@hapto.design");
  const [isFocused, setIsFocused] = useState(false);

  const currentInfo = STATES_INFO[state];
  const isError = state === "error";
  const hasIcon = state === "icon";

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-between gap-4 py-1">
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
          Variação do Campo
        </span>
        <SegmentedControl
          value={state}
          onValueChange={(val) => setState(val as InputState)}
        >
          {(["normal", "icon", "error"] as InputState[]).map((st) => (
            <SegmentedControlItem key={st} value={st}>
              {STATES_INFO[st].label}
            </SegmentedControlItem>
          ))}
        </SegmentedControl>
      </div>

      <div className="w-full max-w-sm px-1 py-1">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-1.5">
          <label
            htmlFor="showcase-input"
            className="text-xs font-bold text-ink"
          >
            {isError ? "E-mail com Erro" : "E-mail de Acesso"}
          </label>
          <span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-muted">min-h-12 (48px)</span>
        </div>

        <Input
          id="showcase-input"
          value={value}
          onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)}
          onFocus={() => setIsFocused(true)}
          onBlur={() => setIsFocused(false)}
          placeholder="Digite algo aqui..."
          startIcon={
            hasIcon ? (
              <Mail
                className={cn(
                  "size-5 transition-colors",
                  isFocused ? "text-primary" : "text-muted"
                )}
              />
            ) : undefined
          }
          endIcon={
            value ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setValue("")}
                aria-label="Limpar campo de texto"
                className="flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-muted transition-colors hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink active:scale-90 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X className="size-4" />
              </button>
            ) : undefined
          }
          error={
            isError
              ? "Formato de e-mail inválido. Verifique o endereço."
              : undefined
          }
          helperText={
            !isError
              ? isFocused
                ? "Digitando... alvo confortável para o polegar."
                : "Toque no campo para interagir."
              : undefined
          }
        />
      </div>

      <div className="w-full rounded-xl border border-line/60 bg-surface/70 p-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-ink">
            <Info className="size-3.5 text-primary" />
            <span>Ergonomia do Input ({currentInfo.label})</span>
          </span>
          <Badge className={cn("shrink-0", currentInfo.badgeColor)}>
            {currentInfo.tag}
          </Badge>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">
          {currentInfo.ergonomicNote}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-between border-t border-line/50 pt-2 text-[11px] text-muted">
        <span className="font-mono text-[10px]">Alvo: 48dp • Fonte: 16px</span>
        <span className="font-medium text-ink">
          {isFocused ? (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-primary animate-in fade-in">
              <Check className="size-3" />
              Campo Focado
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-muted/60">Em repouso</span>
          )}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/catalog/showcases/search-showcase.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  Check,
  Clock,
  Info,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  Sparkles,
} from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  Badge,
  Chip,
  SearchInput,
  SegmentedControl,
  SegmentedControlItem,
} from "@/components/ui";

type SearchVariant = "standard" | "with-filter" | "suggestions";

const VARIATIONS_INFO: Record<
  SearchVariant,
  { label: string; tag: string; ergonomicNote: string; badgeColor: string }
> = {
  standard: {
    label: "Busca + Limpar",
    tag: "Altura 48dp",
    ergonomicNote: "Altura mínima de 48dp calibrada para o polegar e fonte de 16px para prevenir o zoom involuntário no iOS Safari.",
    badgeColor: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-500/30",
  },
  "with-filter": {
    label: "Com Filtro",
    tag: "Ação Composta",
    ergonomicNote: "Botão de filtro incorporado com alvo de toque de 40dp e estado ativo destacado, evitando navegação desnecessária de telas.",
    badgeColor: "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600 border-blue-500/30",
  },
  suggestions: {
    label: "Com Chips Rápidos",
    tag: "Thumb Reach",
    ergonomicNote: "Chips táteis posicionados abaixo do campo de busca na zona natural de alcance, permitindo refinamento com um único toque.",
    badgeColor: "bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-600 border-purple-500/30",
  },
};

export const SEARCH_CODE = `// Exemplo com Hapto Design System
import { useState } from "react";
import { SearchInput, Chip } from "@/components/ui";

export function BarraDeBuscaErgonomica() {
  const [busca, setBusca] = useState("");
  const [filtroAtivo, setFiltroAtivo] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md space-y-2">
      {/* 1. Componente SearchInput com alvo de 48dp para polegar */}
      <SearchInput
        value={busca}
        onChange={(e) => setBusca(e.target.value)}
        onClear={() => setBusca("")}
        filterActive={filtroAtivo}
        onFilterClick={() => setFiltroAtivo(!filtroAtivo)}
        placeholder="Buscar componentes..."
      />

      {/* 2. Chips Rápidos na Thumb Zone */}
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        {["Botões", "Inputs", "Feedback"].map((tag) => (
          <Chip key={tag} size="sm" onClick={() => setBusca(tag)}>
            {tag}
          </Chip>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};`;

const SUGGESTIONS = ["Botões", "Inputs", "Feedback", "48dp"];

export function SearchShowcase() {
  const [variant, setVariant] = useState<SearchVariant>("standard");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("Componente");
  const [filterActive, setFilterActive] = useState(false);
  const [selectedTag, setSelectedTag] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const currentInfo = VARIATIONS_INFO[variant];

  const handleClear = () => {
    setQuery("");
    setSelectedTag(null);
  };

  const handleSelectTag = (tag: string) => {
    if (selectedTag === tag) {
      setSelectedTag(null);
      setQuery("");
    } else {
      setSelectedTag(tag);
      setQuery(tag);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-between gap-4 py-1">
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
          Variação da Barra de Busca
        </span>
        <SegmentedControl
          value={variant}
          onValueChange={(val) => setVariant(val as SearchVariant)}
        >
          {(["standard", "with-filter", "suggestions"] as SearchVariant[]).map((v) => (
            <SegmentedControlItem key={v} value={v}>
              {VARIATIONS_INFO[v].label}
            </SegmentedControlItem>
          ))}
        </SegmentedControl>
      </div>

      <div className="flex min-h-[108px] w-full max-w-sm flex-col justify-center gap-2.5 px-1">
        <SearchInput
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setQuery(e.target.value);
            setSelectedTag(null);
          }}
          onClear={handleClear}
          placeholder="Digite para buscar..."
          endAction={
            variant === "with-filter" ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label="Alternar filtros"
                onClick={() => setFilterActive(!filterActive)}
                className={cn(
                  "mr-1 flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg transition-all active:scale-90 cursor-pointer",
                  filterActive
                    ? "bg-primary text-on-primary shadow-xs"
                    : "text-muted hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink"
                )}
                title={filterActive ? "Filtro ativo" : "Aplicar filtro"}
              >
                <SlidersHorizontal className="size-4" />
              </button>
            ) : undefined
          }
        />

        {variant === "suggestions" && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-0.5 [scrollbar-width:none]">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-semibold text-muted shrink-0 pr-1">
              <Clock className="size-3" />
              <span>Rápido:</span>
            </span>
            {SUGGESTIONS.map((tag) => {
              const isTagSelected = selectedTag === tag;
              return (
                <Chip
                  key={tag}
                  size="sm"
                  selected={isTagSelected}
                  onClick={() => handleSelectTag(tag)}
                >
                  {tag}
                </Chip>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}

        {variant === "with-filter" && filterActive && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-primary">
            <Sparkles className="size-3" />
            <span>Filtro de busca mobile ativado (+ relevância móvel)</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="w-full rounded-xl border border-line/60 bg-surface/70 p-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-ink">
            <Info className="size-3.5 text-primary" />
            <span>Ergonomia ({currentInfo.label})</span>
          </span>
          <Badge className={cn("shrink-0", currentInfo.badgeColor)}>
            {currentInfo.tag}
          </Badge>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">
          {currentInfo.ergonomicNote}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-between border-t border-line/50 pt-2 text-[11px] text-muted">
        <span className="font-mono text-[10px]">
          Alvo: 48dp • Limpar: 36-40dp • Fonte: 16px
        </span>
        <span className="font-medium text-ink flex items-center gap-1">
          {query ? (
            <>
              <Check className="size-3 text-emerald-600" />
              <span className="text-emerald-600 truncate max-w-[140px]">
                &quot;{query}&quot;
              </span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-muted">Aguardando busca</span>
          )}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/catalog/showcases/spacing-showcase.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  AlertTriangle,
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  Info,
} from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

type SpacingVariant = "touch-safety" | "scale-grid" | "edge-gutter";

const VARIATIONS_INFO: Record<
  SpacingVariant,
  { label: string; tag: string; ergonomicNote: string; badgeColor: string }
> = {
  "touch-safety": {
    label: "Respiro entre Alvos (8dp+)",
    tag: "Prevenção de Erro",
    ergonomicNote:
      "Alvos de toque adjacentes exigem no mínimo 8dp (idealmente 12-16dp) de separação para evitar toques acidentais simultâneos com a área de contato do polegar (~10mm).",
    badgeColor: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-500/30",
  },
  "scale-grid": {
    label: "Grid de 8pt / 4pt",
    tag: "Escala Harmônica",
    ergonomicNote:
      "Múltiplos de 4px e 8px alinham perfeitamente com a densidade de pixels (@2x e @3x) dos displays mobile, garantindo nitidez sem frações de pixel.",
    badgeColor: "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600 border-blue-500/30",
  },
  "edge-gutter": {
    label: "Margem de Borda (Gutter)",
    tag: "Zona de Repouso",
    ergonomicNote:
      "Margem externa mínima de 16px protege contra toques involuntários da base da palma e evita conflitos com os gestos de navegação lateral do iOS e Android.",
    badgeColor: "bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-600 border-purple-500/30",
  },
};

export const SPACING_CODE = `// Escala de Espaçamento e Respiro Tátil com Tailwind CSS
// Regra de Ouro: Distância mínima de 8dp entre alvos de toque e margem de borda >= 16dp

export const RESPIRO_ERGONOMICO = {
  // Respiro mínimo obrigatório entre dois botões/ícones táteis (WCAG 2.5.8)
  touchTargetGap: "gap-2",      // 8px (0.5rem) - Mínimo seguro

  // Respiro recomendado para ações confortáveis sem precisão extrema
  comfortableGap: "gap-3",      // 12px (0.75rem) - Ideal para listas/toolbars
  relaxedGap: "gap-4",          // 16px (1rem) - Botões empilhados

  // Margens de tela (Screen Gutters) - Protege contra toques da palma da mão
  screenGutterMobile: "px-4",   // 16px nas bordas em smartphones
  screenGutterTablet: "px-6",   // 24px em telas maiores
};

// Exemplo: Dois botões com respiro seguro contra toques acidentais
export function BotoesComRespiro() {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
      <button className="min-h-11 rounded-xl bg-primary px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-on-primary active:scale-95">
        Salvar
      </button>
      <button className="min-h-11 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-ink active:scale-95">
        Cancelar
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}`;

type GapChoice = "4px" | "8px" | "16px";

const GAP_DATA: Record<
  GapChoice,
  { label: string; px: number; status: "risk" | "pass" | "gold"; badgeText: string }
> = {
  "4px": {
    label: "4px (Inseguro)",
    px: 4,
    status: "risk",
    badgeText: "Risco de Toque Duplo",
  },
  "8px": {
    label: "8px (Mínimo)",
    px: 8,
    status: "pass",
    badgeText: "WCAG 2.5.8 Seguro",
  },
  "16px": {
    label: "16px (Confortável)",
    px: 16,
    status: "gold",
    badgeText: "Padrão Ouro Hapto",
  },
};

type ScaleToken = "4px" | "8px" | "12px" | "16px" | "24px";

const SCALE_TOKENS: Record<
  ScaleToken,
  { token: string; useCase: string }
> = {
  "4px": { token: "space-1 (0.25rem)", useCase: "Micro-respiro de ícone / badge" },
  "8px": { token: "space-2 (0.5rem)", useCase: "Distância mínima entre alvos de toque" },
  "12px": { token: "space-3 (0.75rem)", useCase: "Respiro entre botões secundários" },
  "16px": { token: "space-4 (1rem)", useCase: "Margem lateral mobile (Screen Gutter)" },
  "24px": { token: "space-6 (1.5rem)", useCase: "Separação entre blocos de conteúdo" },
};

export function SpacingShowcase() {
  const [variant, setVariant] = useState<SpacingVariant>("touch-safety");
  const [selectedGap, setSelectedGap] = useState<GapChoice>("8px");
  const [selectedScale, setSelectedScale] = useState<ScaleToken>("8px");
  const [gutterSize, setGutterSize] = useState<"8px" | "16px">("16px");
  const [lastAction, setLastAction] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const currentInfo = VARIATIONS_INFO[variant];
  const gapInfo = GAP_DATA[selectedGap];

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-between gap-4 py-1">
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
          Variação do Token de Espaçamento
        </span>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface p-1 shadow-xs">
          {(["touch-safety", "scale-grid", "edge-gutter"] as SpacingVariant[]).map((v) => {
            const isSelected = variant === v;
            const item = VARIATIONS_INFO[v];
            return (
              <button
                key={v}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setVariant(v);
                  setLastAction(`Variação: ${item.label}`);
                }}
                aria-pressed={isSelected}
                className={cn(
                  "flex min-h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-3 text-xs font-semibold transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary",
                  isSelected
                    ? "bg-primary text-on-primary shadow-xs"
                    : "text-muted hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink"
                )}
              >
                <span>{item.label}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="relative flex h-[142px] w-full max-w-sm flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-3 shadow-xs">
        {variant === "touch-safety" && (
          <div className="flex h-full flex-col justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-muted">Distância entre Alvos:</span>
              <div className="flex gap-1">
                {(["4px", "8px", "16px"] as GapChoice[]).map((g) => (
                  <button
                    key={g}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setSelectedGap(g);
                      setLastAction(`Respiro ajustado para ${g}: ${GAP_DATA[g].badgeText}`);
                    }}
                    className={cn(
                      "rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold transition-all active:scale-95",
                      selectedGap === g
                        ? g === "4px"
                          ? "border-rose-500 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-600 font-bold"
                          : "border-primary bg-primary/10 text-primary font-bold"
                        : "border-line bg-surface text-muted hover:text-ink"
                    )}
                  >
                    {g}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface-raised/70 p-2.5">
              <div
                className="flex items-center transition-all duration-300"
                style={{ gap: `${gapInfo.px}px` }}
              >
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setLastAction(`Botão 'Confirmar' acionado (${selectedGap} gap)`)}
                  className="rounded-lg bg-primary px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-on-primary shadow-2xs transition-transform active:scale-95"
                >
                  Confirmar
                </button>

                <div className="relative flex flex-col items-center">
                  <span className="font-mono text-[9px] font-extrabold text-muted">
                    {gapInfo.px}px
                  </span>
                  <div
                    className={cn(
                      "h-1 rounded-full transition-all duration-300",
                      gapInfo.status === "risk"
                        ? "bg-rose-500"
                        : gapInfo.status === "pass"
                        ? "bg-emerald-500"
                        : "bg-primary"
                    )}
                    style={{ width: `${Math.max(gapInfo.px, 6)}px` }}
                  />
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setLastAction(`Botão 'Excluir' acionado (${selectedGap} gap)`)}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-ink shadow-2xs transition-transform active:scale-95 hover:border-line"
                >
                  Excluir
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px]">
              <span className="text-muted">Status do Toque:</span>
              <span
                className={cn(
                  "flex items-center gap-1 font-bold",
                  gapInfo.status === "risk"
                    ? "text-rose-600"
                    : gapInfo.status === "pass"
                    ? "text-emerald-600"
                    : "text-primary"
                )}
              >
                {gapInfo.status === "risk" ? (
                  <>
                    <AlertTriangle className="size-3" />
                    <span>{gapInfo.badgeText}</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <CheckCircle2 className="size-3" />
                    <span>{gapInfo.badgeText}</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {variant === "scale-grid" && (
          <div className="flex h-full flex-col justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-ink">Tokens de Espaçamento</span>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">Grid Base: 8pt</span>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-5 gap-1.5">
              {(["4px", "8px", "12px", "16px", "24px"] as ScaleToken[]).map((tok) => {
                const isSelected = selectedScale === tok;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={tok}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setSelectedScale(tok);
                      setLastAction(`Token ${tok}: ${SCALE_TOKENS[tok].token}`);
                    }}
                    className={cn(
                      "flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border p-1.5 transition-all active:scale-95",
                      isSelected
                        ? "border-primary bg-primary/10 text-primary shadow-xs"
                        : "border-line bg-surface-raised text-muted hover:border-primary/40 hover:text-ink"
                    )}
                  >
                    <span className="font-mono text-[10px] font-extrabold">{tok}</span>
                    <span className="text-[8px] opacity-75">
                      {tok === "4px" ? "0.5x" : tok === "8px" ? "1x" : tok === "12px" ? "1.5x" : tok === "16px" ? "2x" : "3x"}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface-raised/70 px-2 py-1.5 text-center">
              <p className="truncate text-[10px] text-ink font-medium">
                <strong className="text-primary">{selectedScale}</strong>: {SCALE_TOKENS[selectedScale].useCase}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {variant === "edge-gutter" && (
          <div className="flex h-full flex-col justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-ink">Margem Lateral da Tela</span>
              <div className="flex gap-1">
                {(["8px", "16px"] as const).map((sz) => (
                  <button
                    key={sz}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setGutterSize(sz);
                      setLastAction(`Gutter: ${sz} ${sz === "16px" ? "(Recomendado)" : "(Estreito)"}`);
                    }}
                    className={cn(
                      "rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold transition-all active:scale-95",
                      gutterSize === sz
                        ? sz === "16px"
                          ? "border-primary bg-primary/10 text-primary"
                          : "border-amber-500 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600"
                        : "border-line bg-surface text-muted"
                    )}
                  >
                    {sz} {sz === "16px" && "★"}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="relative flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface-raised/80 p-2">
              <div
                className="flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-dashed border-primary/50 bg-surface py-2 text-center transition-all duration-300"
                style={{
                  paddingLeft: `${gutterSize === "16px" ? 16 : 8}px`,
                  paddingRight: `${gutterSize === "16px" ? 16 : 8}px`,
                }}
              >
                <span className="font-mono text-[9px] text-primary font-bold">
                  {gutterSize}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-ink truncate px-1">
                  Área Segura de Conteúdo
                </span>
                <span className="font-mono text-[9px] text-primary font-bold">
                  {gutterSize}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <p className="truncate text-center text-[10px] text-muted">
              {gutterSize === "16px"
                ? "16px cria o recuo ideal contra toques fantasmas da palma."
                : "8px fica muito rente à borda física do smartphone."}
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="h-[86px] w-full shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line/60 bg-surface/70 p-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 whitespace-nowrap">
          <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-ink">
            <Info className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-primary" />
            <span className="truncate">Ergonomia ({currentInfo.label})</span>
          </span>
          <span
            className={cn(
              "shrink-0 rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider",
              currentInfo.badgeColor
            )}
          >
            {currentInfo.tag}
          </span>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1.5 line-clamp-2 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">
          {currentInfo.ergonomicNote}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex h-[26px] w-full shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-2 whitespace-nowrap border-t border-line/50 pt-2 text-[11px] text-muted">
        <span className="truncate font-mono text-[10px]">
          Alvos: &ge;8dp gap • Gutter: 16px • Grid: 8pt
        </span>
        <span className="flex min-w-0 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 font-medium">
          {lastAction ? (
            <>
              <Check className="size-3 shrink-0 text-emerald-600" />
              <span className="truncate font-semibold text-emerald-600">{lastAction}</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <span className="truncate text-muted">Teste as distâncias e alvos</span>
          )}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Info, Shield } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  Badge,
  Switch,
  Checkbox,
  SwitchRow,
  SegmentedControl,
  SegmentedControlItem,
} from "@/components/ui";

type ControlType = "switch" | "checkbox" | "row";

const VARIATIONS_INFO: Record<
  ControlType,
  { label: string; tag: string; ergonomicNote: string; badgeColor: string }
> = {
  switch: {
    label: "Switch Tátil",
    tag: "Ação Imediata",
    ergonomicNote: "O controle tem corpo de 28px, mas a área tátil do container atinge 48dp para prevenir erros de ativação.",
    badgeColor: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-500/30",
  },
  checkbox: {
    label: "Checkbox 48dp",
    tag: "Seleção Múltipla",
    ergonomicNote: "O label inteiro é clicável (min-h-12). O polegar nunca precisa mirar apenas na caixinha de 20px.",
    badgeColor: "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600 border-blue-500/30",
  },
  row: {
    label: "Linha de Ajuste",
    tag: "Padrão Mobile",
    ergonomicNote: "Estrutura de 56dp com título, descrição e gatilho à direita, otimizada para listas em telas sensíveis ao toque.",
    badgeColor: "bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-600 border-purple-500/30",
  },
};

export const SWITCHES_CODE = `// Exemplo com Hapto Design System
import { useState } from "react";
import { Shield } from "lucide-react";
import { Switch, Checkbox, SwitchRow } from "@/components/ui";

export function ControlesErgonomicos() {
  const [switchAtivo, setSwitchAtivo] = useState(true);
  const [checkAtivo, setCheckAtivo] = useState(false);
  const [rowAtivo, setRowAtivo] = useState(true);

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-3">
      {/* 1. Switch Tátil de 48dp com Label Clicável */}
      <Switch
        checked={switchAtivo}
        onCheckedChange={setSwitchAtivo}
        label="Notificações Push"
        description="Alertas em tempo real"
      />

      {/* 2. Checkbox Ergonômico de 48dp em Formato Card */}
      <Checkbox
        variant="card"
        checked={checkAtivo}
        onCheckedChange={setCheckAtivo}
        label="Feedback Háptico"
        description="Vibração ao interagir"
      />

      {/* 3. Linha de Ajuste (56dp para Polegar) com Ícone */}
      <SwitchRow
        checked={rowAtivo}
        onCheckedChange={setRowAtivo}
        icon={Shield}
        label="Proteção de Toque"
        description="Bloqueio de cliques duplos"
      />
    </div>
  );
};`;

export function SwitchesShowcase() {
  const [type, setType] = useState<ControlType>("switch");
  const [switchOn, setSwitchOn] = useState(true);
  const [checkboxOn, setCheckboxOn] = useState(true);
  const [rowSwitchOn, setRowSwitchOn] = useState(false);

  const currentInfo = VARIATIONS_INFO[type];

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-between gap-4 py-1">
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
          Tipo de Controle Tátil
        </span>
        <SegmentedControl
          value={type}
          onValueChange={(val) => setType(val as ControlType)}
        >
          {(["switch", "checkbox", "row"] as ControlType[]).map((t) => (
            <SegmentedControlItem key={t} value={t}>
              {VARIATIONS_INFO[t].label}
            </SegmentedControlItem>
          ))}
        </SegmentedControl>
      </div>

      <div className="flex min-h-[92px] w-full max-w-sm items-center justify-center px-1">
        {type === "switch" && (
          <div className="w-full">
            <Switch
              checked={switchOn}
              onCheckedChange={setSwitchOn}
              label="Modo Ergonômico"
              description={switchOn ? "Ativo (Foco aumentado)" : "Inativo (Padrão)"}
              className="shadow-2xs"
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {type === "checkbox" && (
          <div className="w-full">
            <Checkbox
              variant="card"
              checked={checkboxOn}
              onCheckedChange={setCheckboxOn}
              label="Habilitar Feedback Háptico"
              description="Toque em qualquer ponto da linha"
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {type === "row" && (
          <div className="w-full">
            <SwitchRow
              checked={rowSwitchOn}
              onCheckedChange={setRowSwitchOn}
              icon={Shield}
              label="Proteção de Toque"
              description="Bloqueio de cliques duplos"
            />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="w-full rounded-xl border border-line/60 bg-surface/70 p-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-ink">
            <Info className="size-3.5 text-primary" />
            <span>Ergonomia ({currentInfo.label})</span>
          </span>
          <Badge className={cn("shrink-0", currentInfo.badgeColor)}>
            {currentInfo.tag}
          </Badge>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">
          {currentInfo.ergonomicNote}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-between border-t border-line/50 pt-2 text-[11px] text-muted">
        <span className="font-mono text-[10px]">Alvo: 48dp • Container Clicável</span>
        <span className="font-medium text-ink">
          {type === "switch" && (switchOn ? "Ligado" : "Desligado")}
          {type === "checkbox" && (checkboxOn ? "Marcado" : "Desmarcado")}
          {type === "row" && (rowSwitchOn ? "Protegido" : "Desprotegido")}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  Check,
  CheckSquare,
  Info,
  MoreVertical,
  Search,
  Share2,
  Trash2,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  Badge,
  SegmentedControl,
  SegmentedControlItem,
  TopBar,
  TopBarAction,
  TopBarTitle,
  TopBarActions,
  TopBarSection,
  TopBarNavRow,
  TopBarLargeTitleArea,
  type TopBarVariant,
} from "@/components/ui";

const VARIATIONS_INFO: Record<
  TopBarVariant,
  { label: string; tag: string; ergonomicNote: string; badgeColor: string }
> = {
  standard: {
    label: "Padrão (Voltar + Ações)",
    tag: "Hitbox 48dp",
    ergonomicNote: "Botão de voltar com área de toque mínima de 48dp na zona superior, com respiro de 12px entre ícones à direita para evitar toques simultâneos involuntários.",
    badgeColor: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-500/30",
  },
  "large-title": {
    label: "Título Expandido",
    tag: "Escaneabilidade",
    ergonomicNote: "Tipografia em destaque traz o ponto focal visual para mais perto do centro da tela, facilitando a leitura inicial sem esticar o polegar.",
    badgeColor: "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600 border-blue-500/30",
  },
  contextual: {
    label: "Barra Contextual",
    tag: "Ações em Lote",
    ergonomicNote: "Modo seleção rápida substitui a navegação com botão 'Cancelar' explícito e isola ações destrutivas na borda oposta com feedback imediato.",
    badgeColor: "bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-600 border-purple-500/30",
  },
};

export const TOP_BAR_CODE = `// Exemplo com Hapto Design System
import { ArrowLeft, Share2, MoreVertical } from "lucide-react";
import { TopBar, TopBarAction, TopBarTitle, TopBarActions } from "@/components/ui";

export function TopAppBarErgonomica() {
  return (
    <TopBar variant="standard" position="sticky">
      {/* 1. Botão Voltar com hitbox calibrada de 48dp */}
      <TopBarAction
        icon={ArrowLeft}
        aria-label="Voltar à tela anterior"
        onClick={() => history.back()}
      />

      {/* 2. Título Central com Truncamento Seguro */}
      <TopBarTitle align="center">
        Detalhes do Componente
      </TopBarTitle>

      {/* 3. Ações Secundárias com Respiro Ergonômico */}
      <TopBarActions>
        <TopBarAction icon={Share2} aria-label="Compartilhar" />
        <TopBarAction icon={MoreVertical} aria-label="Mais opções" />
      </TopBarActions>
    </TopBar>
  );
};`;

export function TopBarShowcase() {
  const [variant, setVariant] = useState<TopBarVariant>("standard");
  const [selectedCount, setSelectedCount] = useState(3);
  const [lastAction, setLastAction] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const currentInfo = VARIATIONS_INFO[variant];

  const triggerAction = (msg: string) => {
    setLastAction(msg);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-between gap-4 py-1">
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
          Variação do Organismo
        </span>
        <SegmentedControl
          value={variant}
          onValueChange={(val) => {
            const v = val as TopBarVariant;
            setVariant(v);
            setLastAction(`Variação selecionada: ${VARIATIONS_INFO[v].label}`);
          }}
        >
          {(["standard", "large-title", "contextual"] as TopBarVariant[]).map((v) => (
            <SegmentedControlItem key={v} value={v}>
              {VARIATIONS_INFO[v].label}
            </SegmentedControlItem>
          ))}
        </SegmentedControl>
      </div>

      <div className="relative flex h-[142px] w-full max-w-sm flex-col justify-start overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface shadow-xs">
        {variant === "standard" && (
          <TopBar variant="standard">
            <TopBarAction
              aria-label="Voltar"
              icon={ArrowLeft}
              onClick={() => triggerAction("Ação: Voltar acionado")}
            />

            <TopBarTitle>Design Systems</TopBarTitle>

            <TopBarActions>
              <TopBarAction
                variant="muted"
                aria-label="Compartilhar"
                icon={Share2}
                onClick={() => triggerAction("Ação: Compartilhar aberto")}
              />
              <TopBarAction
                variant="muted"
                aria-label="Mais opções"
                icon={MoreVertical}
                onClick={() => triggerAction("Ação: Menu de opções aberto")}
              />
            </TopBarActions>
          </TopBar>
        )}

        {variant === "large-title" && (
          <TopBar variant="large-title">
            <TopBarNavRow>
              <TopBarAction
                aria-label="Voltar"
                icon={ArrowLeft}
                onClick={() => triggerAction("Ação: Voltar acionado")}
              />

              <TopBarActions>
                <TopBarAction
                  variant="muted"
                  aria-label="Buscar"
                  icon={Search}
                  onClick={() => triggerAction("Ação: Busca aberta")}
                />
              </TopBarActions>
            </TopBarNavRow>

            <TopBarLargeTitleArea>
              <TopBarTitle variant="large">Componentes</TopBarTitle>
            </TopBarLargeTitleArea>
          </TopBar>
        )}

        {variant === "contextual" && (
          <TopBar variant="contextual">
            <TopBarSection gap="normal">
              <TopBarAction
                variant="primary"
                aria-label="Fechar seleção"
                icon={X}
                onClick={() => triggerAction("Seleção cancelada")}
              />
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-primary">
                {selectedCount} selecionados
              </span>
            </TopBarSection>

            <TopBarActions>
              <TopBarAction
                variant="primary"
                aria-label="Selecionar todos"
                title="Selecionar todos"
                icon={CheckSquare}
                onClick={() => {
                  setSelectedCount(5);
                  triggerAction("Todos (5) selecionados");
                }}
              />
              <TopBarAction
                variant="destructive"
                aria-label="Excluir selecionados"
                title="Excluir"
                icon={Trash2}
                onClick={() => {
                  setSelectedCount(0);
                  triggerAction("Itens excluídos com segurança");
                }}
              />
            </TopBarActions>
          </TopBar>
        )}

        <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center p-3 text-center">
          <p className="text-[11px] font-medium text-ink">
            {variant === "standard" && "Corpo da Tela: rolagem sob o cabeçalho"}
            {variant === "large-title" && "Área de leitura primária acessível"}
            {variant === "contextual" && "Modo de ação contextual ativado"}
          </p>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">
            Toque nos ícones da Top Bar para testar hitboxes
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="h-[86px] w-full shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line/60 bg-surface/70 p-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 whitespace-nowrap">
          <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-ink">
            <Info className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-primary" />
            <span className="truncate">Ergonomia ({currentInfo.label})</span>
          </span>
          <Badge className={cn("shrink-0", currentInfo.badgeColor)}>
            {currentInfo.tag}
          </Badge>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1.5 line-clamp-2 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">
          {currentInfo.ergonomicNote}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex h-[26px] w-full shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-2 whitespace-nowrap border-t border-line/50 pt-2 text-[11px] text-muted">
        <span className="truncate font-mono text-[10px]">
          Alvo: 44-48dp • Espaço entre Ações: 8dp
        </span>
        <span className="flex min-w-0 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 font-medium">
          {lastAction ? (
            <>
              <Check className="size-3 shrink-0 text-emerald-600" />
              <span className="truncate font-semibold text-emerald-600">{lastAction}</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <span className="truncate text-muted">Toque em uma ação para testar</span>
          )}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/catalog/showcases/typography-showcase.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  Info,
  Smartphone,
  ZoomIn,
} from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

type TypographyVariant = "ios-zoom-guard" | "scale-hierarchy" | "line-height-thumb";

const VARIATIONS_INFO: Record<
  TypographyVariant,
  { label: string; tag: string; ergonomicNote: string; badgeColor: string }
> = {
  "ios-zoom-guard": {
    label: "Regra dos 16px (iOS)",
    tag: "Prevenção de Zoom",
    ergonomicNote: "No iOS Safari, campos de texto com fonte abaixo de 16px disparam o zoom automático da tela ao focar, quebrando a viewport e desorientando o usuário.",
    badgeColor: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-500/30",
  },
  "scale-hierarchy": {
    label: "Escala Mobile",
    tag: "Escaneabilidade",
    ergonomicNote: "Escala tipográfica proporcional ao tamanho do display portátil. Evita fontes exageradas que quebram títulos em várias linhas na rolagem do polegar.",
    badgeColor: "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600 border-blue-500/30",
  },
  "line-height-thumb": {
    label: "Respiro de Linha",
    tag: "Leitura em Rolagem",
    ergonomicNote: "Line-height calibrado em 1.5x a 1.6x para textos corridos impede que as linhas 'pulem' ou se misturem durante a rolagem rápida com o polegar.",
    badgeColor: "bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-600 border-purple-500/30",
  },
};

export const TYPOGRAPHY_CODE = `// Escala Tipográfica Ergonômica Mobile-First com Tailwind CSS
// Regra de Ouro: Campos de texto sempre com 'text-base' (16px) para evitar auto-zoom no iOS Safari

export const TIPOGRAFIA_HAPTO = {
  // Display Mobile: Títulos de topo (com leading compacto para não ocupar meia tela)
  display: "text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight leading-[1.15]",

  // Heading Mobile: Cabeçalhos de seção e cards
  heading: "text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-ink",

  // Body Mobile: Padrão de leitura confortável
  body: "text-base font-normal leading-relaxed text-ink",

  // Input Mobile: MÍNIMO 16px para prevenir auto-zoom no Safari iOS
  input: "text-base text-ink placeholder:text-muted",

  // Caption / Microcopy: Metadados, badges e datas
  caption: "text-xs font-medium text-muted leading-normal",
};

// Exemplo de Input Imune ao Zoom no iOS Safari:
export function InputSemZoom() {
  return (
    <input
      type="text"
      placeholder="Mínimo 16px (text-base)"
      className="min-h-12 w-full rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface px-3.5 text-base text-ink focus:outline-primary"
    />
  );
}`;

export function TypographyShowcase() {
  const [variant, setVariant] = useState<TypographyVariant>("ios-zoom-guard");
  const [testedSize, setTestedSize] = useState<"16px" | "14px">("16px");
  const [selectedScale, setSelectedScale] = useState<"display" | "heading" | "body" | "caption">("body");
  const [lastAction, setLastAction] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const currentInfo = VARIATIONS_INFO[variant];

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-between gap-4 py-1">
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
          Variação Tipográfica
        </span>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface p-1 shadow-xs">
          {(["ios-zoom-guard", "scale-hierarchy", "line-height-thumb"] as TypographyVariant[]).map((v) => {
            const isSelected = variant === v;
            const item = VARIATIONS_INFO[v];
            return (
              <button
                key={v}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setVariant(v);
                  setLastAction(`Variação: ${item.label}`);
                }}
                aria-pressed={isSelected}
                className={cn(
                  "flex min-h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-3 text-xs font-semibold transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary",
                  isSelected
                    ? "bg-primary text-on-primary shadow-xs"
                    : "text-muted hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink"
                )}
              >
                <span>{item.label}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="relative flex h-[142px] w-full max-w-sm flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-3 shadow-xs">
        {variant === "ios-zoom-guard" && (
          <div className="flex h-full flex-col justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-ink">Teste de Foco em Input</span>
              <div className="flex gap-1">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setTestedSize("16px");
                    setLastAction("16px selecionado: Zoom prevenido ✅");
                  }}
                  className={cn(
                    "rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold transition-all active:scale-95",
                    testedSize === "16px"
                      ? "border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600"
                      : "border-line bg-surface text-muted"
                  )}
                >
                  16px (Hapto)
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setTestedSize("14px");
                    setLastAction("14px selecionado: Safari dará zoom involuntário ⚠️");
                  }}
                  className={cn(
                    "rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold transition-all active:scale-95",
                    testedSize === "14px"
                      ? "border-amber-500 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600"
                      : "border-line bg-surface text-muted"
                  )}
                >
                  14px (Risco)
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div
              className={cn(
                "flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border p-2.5 transition-all",
                testedSize === "16px"
                  ? "border-line bg-surface-raised"
                  : "border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/10"
              )}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Smartphone className="size-4 text-muted shrink-0" />
                <span
                  className={cn(
                    "font-medium text-ink transition-all",
                    testedSize === "16px" ? "text-base" : "text-sm scale-95"
                  )}
                >
                  {testedSize === "16px" ? "Fonte: 16px (1rem)" : "Fonte: 14px (0.875rem)"}
                </span>
              </div>

              {testedSize === "16px" ? (
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-600">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="size-3.5" />
                  <span>Sem Zoom</span>
                </span>
              ) : (
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-600 animate-pulse">
                  <ZoomIn className="size-3.5" />
                  <span>Auto-Zoom!</span>
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            <p className="truncate text-center text-[10px] text-muted">
              {testedSize === "16px"
                ? "Ideal: o Safari mantém a viewport intacta ao tocar."
                : "Abaixo de 16px o iOS Safari força o zoom na página."}
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {variant === "scale-hierarchy" && (
          <div className="flex h-full flex-col justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-muted">Nível:</span>
              <div className="flex gap-1">
                {(["display", "heading", "body", "caption"] as const).map((lvl) => (
                  <button
                    key={lvl}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setSelectedScale(lvl);
                      setLastAction(`Escala: ${lvl.toUpperCase()}`);
                    }}
                    className={cn(
                      "rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase transition-all active:scale-95",
                      selectedScale === lvl
                        ? "border-primary bg-primary/10 text-primary"
                        : "border-line bg-surface text-muted hover:text-ink"
                    )}
                  >
                    {lvl}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex min-h-[50px] items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface-raised p-2 text-center">
              {selectedScale === "display" && (
                <span className="text-xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-ink">
                  Display Mobile (24-30px)
                </span>
              )}
              {selectedScale === "heading" && (
                <span className="text-base font-bold text-ink">
                  Heading de Seção (18px)
                </span>
              )}
              {selectedScale === "body" && (
                <span className="text-sm font-normal text-ink leading-relaxed">
                  Corpo de Leitura Calibrado (16px)
                </span>
              )}
              {selectedScale === "caption" && (
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted">
                  Caption & Metadados (12-13px)
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-muted">
              <span>Proporção equilibrada</span>
              <span>Evita quebra em mobile</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {variant === "line-height-thumb" && (
          <div className="flex h-full flex-col justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-ink">Respiro de Linha (1.5x)</span>
              <span className="rounded bg-emerald-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold text-emerald-600">
                Leading Confortável
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface-raised p-2 text-left">
              <p className="text-[11px] font-medium leading-relaxed text-ink line-clamp-3">
                Linhas com espaçamento vertical de 1.5x a 1.6x garantem que os olhos não percam o ponto focal durante a rolagem vertical rápida feita pelo polegar.
              </p>
            </div>

            <p className="truncate text-center text-[10px] text-muted">
              Espaçamento vertical previne confusão tátil e cansaço visual
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="h-[86px] w-full shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line/60 bg-surface/70 p-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 whitespace-nowrap">
          <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-ink">
            <Info className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-primary" />
            <span className="truncate">Ergonomia ({currentInfo.label})</span>
          </span>
          <span
            className={cn(
              "shrink-0 rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider",
              currentInfo.badgeColor
            )}
          >
            {currentInfo.tag}
          </span>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1.5 line-clamp-2 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">
          {currentInfo.ergonomicNote}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex h-[26px] w-full shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-2 whitespace-nowrap border-t border-line/50 pt-2 text-[11px] text-muted">
        <span className="truncate font-mono text-[10px]">
          Base: 16px (1rem) • Leading: 1.5x • Sem Zoom iOS
        </span>
        <span className="flex min-w-0 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 font-medium">
          {lastAction ? (
            <>
              <Check className="size-3 shrink-0 text-emerald-600" />
              <span className="truncate font-semibold text-emerald-600">{lastAction}</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <span className="truncate text-muted">Teste as variações tipográficas</span>
          )}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

          </div>
        </div>
      </aside>
    </Reveal>
