                          Acessar Componentes
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="border-t border-line/60 pt-3 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                    <span className="text-muted">Hierarquia modular:</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-primary">
                      {current.step === 1 && "Átomos · Nível 1"}
                      {current.step === 2 && "Moléculas · Nível 2"}
                      {current.step === 3 && "Organismos · Nível 3"}
                      {current.step === 4 && "Modelos · Nível 4"}
                      {current.step === 5 && "Páginas · Nível 5"}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </Reveal>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

                        {item.title}
                      </h3>
                      <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-primary">
                        {item.tagline}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <ul className="mt-6 space-y-4">
                      {item.points.map((pt) => {
                        const PointIcon = pt.icon;
                        return (
                          <li key={pt.title} className="flex items-start gap-2.5 text-xs text-muted leading-relaxed">
                            <PointIcon className="size-4 shrink-0 text-primary mt-0.5" />
                            <div>
                              <strong className="text-ink font-semibold">{pt.title} </strong>
                              <span>{pt.text}</span>
                            </div>
                          </li>
                        );
                      })}
                    </ul>
                  </div>

                  <div className="mt-8 rounded-xl border border-line/60 bg-surface-raised p-3 text-center">
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-primary">
                      {item.summary}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </article>
              </Reveal>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line/60 pb-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <span className="size-2 rounded-full bg-red-400" />
                      <span className="size-2 rounded-full bg-amber-400" />
                      <span className="size-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400" />
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-muted">
                      {tab === "sem" ? "inspeção // sem-sistema.tsx" : "inspeção // hapto-tokens.tsx"}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-muted/70 hidden sm:inline">
                      375 × 812 dp
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-rows-3 gap-3.5 my-4 flex-1">
                    {tab === "sem" ? (
                      <>
                        <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-red-500/40 bg-surface p-3.5 flex flex-col justify-between h-full">
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-muted">
                            <span className="font-mono font-medium text-ink">Botão Checkout</span>
                            <span className="font-mono text-red-500 font-semibold">{"h: 31px (Inseguro < 48dp)"}</span>
                          </div>
                          <div className="h-11 flex items-center justify-center">
                            <div className="h-7.5 w-full rounded bg-ink/75 flex items-center justify-center text-[11px] font-medium text-surface border border-red-500/30">
                              Confirmar Pedido
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-red-500/40 bg-surface p-3.5 flex flex-col justify-between h-full">
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-muted">
                            <span className="font-mono font-medium text-ink">Input Cupom</span>
                            <span className="font-mono text-red-500 font-semibold">pad: 3px (Sem grid 8dp)</span>
                          </div>
                          <div className="h-11 flex items-center justify-center">
                            <div className="h-7.5 w-full rounded border border-line bg-surface-raised px-2.5 flex items-center text-[11px] text-muted">
                              Insira o cupom...
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-red-500/40 bg-surface p-3.5 flex flex-col justify-between h-full">
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-muted">
                            <span className="font-mono font-medium text-ink">Ação Secundária</span>
                            <span className="font-mono text-red-500 font-semibold">cor: #4f46e5 (Fora do token)</span>
                          </div>
                          <div className="h-11 flex items-center justify-center">
                            <div className="h-9 w-full rounded-full bg-indigo-600 flex items-center justify-center text-[11px] font-medium text-white shadow-2xs">
                              Voltar à Loja
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </>
                    ) : (
                      <>
                        <div className="rounded-xl border border-primary/30 bg-surface p-3.5 shadow-2xs flex flex-col justify-between h-full">
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-muted">
                            <span className="font-mono font-medium text-ink">Button Primary</span>

                            </div>
                          </div>

  cacheLife("days");
  return <>{new Date().getFullYear()}</>;
}

================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/landing/navbar.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { Logo } from "@/components/logo";
import { ThemeToggle } from "@/components/theme-toggle";
import { BookOpen, Palette, Terminal } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const modules = [
  {
    anchorHref: "#fundamentos",
    pageHref: "/fundamentals",
    label: "Início & Fundamento",
    icon: BookOpen,
  },
  {
    anchorHref: "#componentes",
    pageHref: "/components",
    label: "Catálogo de Componentes",
    icon: Palette,
  },
  {
    anchorHref: "#playground",
    pageHref: "/playground",
    label: "Playground",
    icon: Terminal,
  },
];

export function Navbar({ fluid = false }: { fluid?: boolean } = {}) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const isHome = pathname === "/";

  const handleScroll = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>, href: string) => {
    if (href.startsWith("#")) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const target = document.querySelector(href);
      if (target) {
        target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
        history.pushState(null, "", href);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleLogoClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) => {
    if (isHome) {
      e.preventDefault();
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
      history.pushState(null, "", "/");
    }
  };

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-50 w-full border-b border-line/60 bg-bg/85 backdrop-blur-md">
      <div
        className={cn(
          "mx-auto flex h-16 items-center justify-between px-6 transition-all",
          fluid ? "w-full max-w-[1600px] lg:px-8 xl:px-12" : "max-w-6xl"
        )}
      >
        <Link
          href="/"
          onClick={handleLogoClick}
          className="transition hover:opacity-80 shrink-0"
          aria-label="Hapto início"
        >
          <Logo />
        </Link>

        <nav className="hidden items-center md:flex" aria-label="Módulos do Design System">
          <ul className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-line bg-surface/70 p-1 shadow-xs">
            {modules.map(({ anchorHref, pageHref, label }, index) => {
              const targetHref = isHome ? anchorHref : pageHref;
              const isActive = !isHome && pathname === pageHref;

              return (
                <li key={pageHref}>
                  {isHome ? (
                    <a
                      href={targetHref}
                      onClick={(e) => handleScroll(e, targetHref)}
                      className={cn(
                        "flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-muted transition-all hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink active:scale-95",
                        isActive && "bg-surface-raised text-ink"
                      )}
                    >
                      <span className="flex size-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary-light text-[10px] font-bold text-primary">
                        {index + 1}
                      </span>
                      <span>{label}</span>
                    </a>
                  ) : (
                    <Link
                      href={targetHref}
                      className={cn(
                        "flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-muted transition-all hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink active:scale-95",
                        isActive && "bg-surface-raised text-ink shadow-2xs font-bold"
                      )}
                    >
                      <span
                        className={cn(
                          "flex size-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold",
                          isActive
                            ? "bg-primary text-on-primary"
                            : "bg-primary-light text-primary"
                        )}
                      >
                        {index + 1}
                      </span>
                      <span>{label}</span>
                    </Link>
                  )}
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        </nav>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
          <ThemeToggle />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="border-t border-line/40 bg-surface/40 px-3 py-1.5 md:hidden">
        <ul className="flex items-center justify-around gap-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-muted">
          {modules.map(({ anchorHref, pageHref, label }, idx) => {
            const targetHref = isHome ? anchorHref : pageHref;
            const isActive = !isHome && pathname === pageHref;

            const linkClasses = cn(
              "flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95",
              isActive
                ? "bg-surface-raised font-bold text-primary shadow-2xs"
                : "hover:text-ink active:bg-surface-raised"
            );

            return (
              <li key={pageHref} className="flex-1">
                {isHome ? (
                  <a
                    href={targetHref}
                    onClick={(e) => handleScroll(e, targetHref)}
                    className={linkClasses}
                  >
                    <span className="font-bold text-primary">{idx + 1}.</span>
                    <span className="truncate">{label.split(" ")[0]}</span>
                  </a>
                ) : (
                  <Link href={targetHref} className={linkClasses}>
                    <span className="font-bold text-primary">{idx + 1}.</span>
                    <span className="truncate">{label.split(" ")[0]}</span>
                  </Link>
                )}
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

import type { HandGrip } from "../types";
import { Hand } from "lucide-react";
import {
  Badge,
  SegmentedControl,
  SegmentedControlItem,
} from "@/components/ui";

interface HandSelectorProps {
  value: HandGrip;
  onChange: (value: HandGrip) => void;
}

export function HandSelector({ value, onChange }: HandSelectorProps) {
  const options: { id: HandGrip; label: string; sub: string }[] = [
    { id: "left", label: "Canhoto", sub: "Mão esquerda" },
    { id: "right", label: "Destro", sub: "Mão direita" },
    { id: "both", label: "Duas Mãos", sub: "Dois polegares" },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
          <Hand className="size-3.5 text-primary" />
          <span>Pegada da Mão</span>
        </label>
        <Badge variant="primary" shape="pill" size="sm">
          {value === "right" ? "Destro" : value === "left" ? "Canhoto" : "2 Mãos"}
        </Badge>
      </div>

      <SegmentedControl
        value={value}
        onValueChange={(val) => onChange(val as HandGrip)}
        variant="raised"
        itemVariant="surface"
        fullWidth
        className="grid grid-cols-3"
      >
        {options.map((option) => (
          <SegmentedControlItem
            key={option.id}
            value={option.id}
            detail={option.sub}
            className="flex-col py-2 min-h-12"
          >
            {option.label}
          </SegmentedControlItem>
        ))}
      </SegmentedControl>
    </div>
  );
}

import type { TargetSize } from "../types";
import { CheckCircle2, Crosshair } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface TargetCalibratorProps {
  value: TargetSize;
  onChange: (value: TargetSize) => void;
}

export function TargetCalibrator({ value, onChange }: TargetCalibratorProps) {
  const options: {
    id: TargetSize;
    label: string;
    detail: string;
    badge: string;
    badgeColor: string;
  }[] = [
    {
      id: "40px",
      label: "40px",
      detail: "Alvo reduzido — alto risco de erro no toque do polegar",
      badge: "Subdimensionado",
      badgeColor: "bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-600 border-rose-500/20",
    },
    {
      id: "44px",
      label: "44px",
      detail: "Mínimo estipulado pela Apple (Human Interface Guidelines)",
      badge: "Mínimo Apple",
      badgeColor: "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 border-amber-500/20",
    },
    {
      id: "48px",
      label: "48px",
      detail: "Padrão ouro ergonômico (Material Design & Hapto)",
      badge: "Padrão Ouro",
      badgeColor: "bg-primary-light text-primary border-primary/30",
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
          <Crosshair className="size-3.5 text-primary" />
          <span>Calibragem de Target Tátil</span>
        </label>
        <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-muted">Lei de Fitts</span>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        {options.map((option) => {
          const isSelected = value === option.id;
          return (
            <button
              key={option.id}
              type="button"
              aria-pressed={isSelected}
              onClick={() => onChange(option.id)}
              className={cn(
                "group flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-xl border p-2.5 text-left transition-all cursor-pointer min-h-[58px]",
                isSelected
                  ? "border-primary bg-primary-light/30 shadow-xs ring-1 ring-primary/30"
                  : "border-line bg-surface hover:border-line hover:bg-surface-raised/50"
              )}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                <div
                  className={cn(
                    "flex size-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-colors",
                    isSelected
                      ? "bg-primary text-on-primary"
                      : "bg-surface-raised text-muted group-hover:text-ink"
                  )}
                >
                  {option.label}
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink">
                      Altura {option.label}
                    </span>
                    <span
                      className={cn(
                        "rounded px-1.5 py-0.2 text-[9px] font-semibold border",
                        option.badgeColor
                      )}
                    >
                      {option.badge}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-muted leading-tight mt-0.5 truncate">
                    {option.detail}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <CheckCircle2
                className={cn(
                  "size-4 text-primary shrink-0 ml-2 transition-opacity",
                  isSelected ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0"
                )}
              />
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { Layers } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface ZoneToggleProps {
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: (checked: boolean) => void;
}

export function ZoneToggle({ checked, onChange }: ZoneToggleProps) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface p-3.5 shadow-2xs space-y-3">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-primary-light text-primary">
            <Layers className="size-4" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-ink">Thumb Zone Overlay</p>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-muted">
              Mapa de alcance do polegar
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          role="switch"
          aria-checked={checked}
          onClick={() => onChange(!checked)}
          className={cn(
            "relative inline-flex h-6 w-11 shrink-0 cursor-pointer rounded-full border-2 border-transparent transition-colors duration-200 ease-in-out focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary",
            checked ? "bg-primary" : "bg-line"
          )}
        >
          <span
            className={cn(
              "pointer-events-none inline-block size-5 transform rounded-full bg-white shadow-md ring-0 transition duration-200 ease-in-out",
              checked ? "translate-x-5" : "translate-x-0"
            )}
          />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-line/50 pt-2 text-[10px] font-semibold">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span
            className={cn(
              "size-2 rounded-full transition-colors",
              checked ? "bg-primary ring-2 ring-primary/20" : "bg-muted/30"
            )}
          />
          <span className={cn("transition-colors", checked ? "text-primary font-bold" : "text-muted/60")}>
            Natural
          </span>
        </div>
        <span className="text-line text-[9px]">•</span>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span
            className={cn(
              "size-2 rounded-full transition-colors",
              checked ? "bg-amber-500 ring-2 ring-amber-500/20" : "bg-muted/30"
            )}
          />
          <span className={cn("transition-colors", checked ? "text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 font-bold" : "text-muted/60")}>
            Esticada
          </span>
        </div>
        <span className="text-line text-[9px]">•</span>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span
            className={cn(
              "size-2 rounded-full transition-colors",
              checked ? "bg-rose-500 ring-2 ring-rose-500/20" : "bg-muted/30"
            )}
          />
          <span className={cn("transition-colors", checked ? "text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 font-bold" : "text-muted/60")}>
            Difícil
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}




================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/playground/diagnostics/touch-feedback.tsx
================================================================================

import type { TargetSize, TouchEventData } from "../types";
import {
  Activity,
  CheckCircle2,
  Fingerprint,
  Info,
  MousePointerClick,
  Sparkles,
} from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface TouchFeedbackProps {
  lastTouch: TouchEventData | null;
  targetSize: TargetSize;
}

export function TouchFeedback({ lastTouch, targetSize }: TouchFeedbackProps) {
  const getZoneEvaluation = (zone: TouchEventData["zone"]) => {
    switch (zone) {
      case "natural":
        return {
          title: "Zona Natural",
          badge: "Confortável",
          badgeColor: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-500/30",
          barColor: "bg-emerald-500",
          effortPercent: 15,
          effortLabel: "Baixo (15% de fadiga motora)",
          description:
            "Excelente posição ergonômica. O polegar toca sem rotação de punho nem troca de empunhadura do aparelho.",
          recommendation: "Ideal para botões de CTA primário, abas de navegação e FAB.",
        };
      case "stretch":
        return {
          title: "Zona Esticada",
          badge: "Alcance Moderado",
          badgeColor: "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 border-amber-500/30",
          barColor: "bg-amber-500",
          effortPercent: 55,
          effortLabel: "Médio (55% de esforço muscular)",
          description:
            "Requer esticar a articulação do polegar. Em telas acima de 6.1\", pode começar a exigir micro-ajuste na mão.",
          recommendation: "Adequado para cartões de conteúdo e botões secundários ocasionais.",
        };
      case "hard":
        return {
          title: "Zona Difícil",
          badge: "Alto Esforço",
          badgeColor: "bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-600 border-rose-500/30",
          barColor: "bg-rose-500",
          effortPercent: 90,
          effortLabel: "Crítico (90% de esforço / risco de queda)",
          description:
            "Força o usuário a deslizar o aparelho na palma da mão ou recorrer à segunda mão para alcançar o topo.",
          recommendation: "Reserve apenas para status, leitura passiva ou ações de rara execução.",
        };
    }
  };

  const targetEvaluation = {
    "40px": {
      tag: "Alto risco de erro",
      badgeColor: "bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-600 border-rose-500/20",
      description: "Abaixo da largura do dedo (~10mm). Aumenta toque duplo acidental.",
    },
    "44px": {
      tag: "Mínimo aceitável",
      badgeColor: "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 border-amber-500/20",
      description: "Padrão Apple HIG. Aceitável, mas requer precisão visual.",
    },
    "48px": {
      tag: "Padrão Ouro (48dp)",
      badgeColor: "bg-primary-light text-primary border-primary/30",
      description: "Ergonomia ideal. Garante toque confortável sem miss-click.",
    },
  }[targetSize];

  return (
    <div className="h-[610px] sm:h-[640px] w-full rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-5 shadow-xs flex flex-col justify-between select-none">
      <div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line/50 pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-primary-light text-primary">
              <Activity className="size-4" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-ink">Diagnóstico Tátil</h3>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-muted">Métricas de alcance em tempo real</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-surface-raised border border-line px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-ink">
            <span className="size-2 rounded-full bg-primary animate-pulse" />
            Ativo
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-3.5 flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl bg-surface-raised/60 border border-line/60 p-3 h-[60px]">
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
              Target Calibrado
            </span>
            <p className="text-base font-extrabold text-ink leading-tight">{targetSize}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="text-right">
            <span
              className={cn(
                "inline-block rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold",
                targetEvaluation.badgeColor
              )}
            >
              {targetEvaluation.tag}
            </span>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-muted mt-0.5 truncate max-w-[190px]">
              {targetEvaluation.description}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-xl border border-line/70 bg-surface-raised/30 p-4 space-y-3 h-[290px] flex flex-col justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line/40 pb-2">
          <h4 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Fingerprint className="size-3.5 text-primary" />
            <span>Último Toque</span>
          </h4>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-muted font-medium">
            {lastTouch ? "Registrado" : "Aguardando"}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-lg border border-line/60 bg-surface p-2.5 space-y-1.5 h-[62px] flex flex-col justify-center">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[11px] text-muted">Elemento:</span>
            <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-ink flex items-center gap-1 truncate max-w-[180px]">
              <MousePointerClick className="size-3 text-primary shrink-0" />
              {lastTouch ? lastTouch.elementClicked : "Nenhum toque"}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[11px] text-muted">Zona:</span>
            {lastTouch ? (
              <span
                className={cn(
                  "rounded px-1.5 py-0.2 text-[9px] font-bold border",
                  getZoneEvaluation(lastTouch.zone).badgeColor
                )}
              >
                {getZoneEvaluation(lastTouch.zone).title}
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span className="rounded bg-line/40 px-1.5 py-0.2 text-[9px] font-medium text-muted">
                Toque no celular
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1 h-[42px] flex flex-col justify-center">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px]">
            <span className="font-semibold text-muted">Fadiga Motora</span>
            <span className="font-bold text-ink">
              {lastTouch
                ? getZoneEvaluation(lastTouch.zone).effortLabel
                : "0% — Sem esforço"}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="h-2 w-full rounded-full bg-surface-raised border border-line/60 overflow-hidden">
            <div
              className={cn(
                "h-full transition-all duration-300 rounded-full",
                lastTouch
                  ? getZoneEvaluation(lastTouch.zone).barColor
                  : "bg-transparent"
              )}
              style={{
                width: `${lastTouch ? getZoneEvaluation(lastTouch.zone).effortPercent : 0}%`,
              }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-lg border border-line/50 bg-surface p-2.5 h-[105px] flex flex-col justify-between text-[11px]">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-1.5 text-muted leading-relaxed line-clamp-2">
            <Info className="size-3 text-primary mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
            <span>
              {lastTouch
                ? getZoneEvaluation(lastTouch.zone).description
                : "Toque em qualquer botão, aba ou campo dentro do celular simulado para ver a análise de ergonomia."}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-start gap-1.5 text-primary text-[10px] font-semibold pt-1 border-t border-line/40 line-clamp-1">
            <Sparkles className="size-3 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <span>
              {lastTouch
                ? getZoneEvaluation(lastTouch.zone).recommendation
                : "Elementos na base reduzem o tempo de reação e o risco de miss-click."}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-xl border border-primary/20 bg-primary/[0.04] p-3 text-xs space-y-1 h-[88px] flex flex-col justify-center">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-primary text-[11px]">
          <CheckCircle2 className="size-3.5 shrink-0" />
          <span>Regra dos 75% da Steven Hoober</span>
        </div>
        <p className="text-[10px] text-muted leading-relaxed line-clamp-2">
          Mais de 75% das interações mobile ocorrem com o polegar de uma só mão. Posicionar alvos na base previne quedas do aparelho e esforço articular.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import type { HandGrip } from "../types";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface ThumbOverlayProps {
  handGrip: HandGrip;
}

export function ThumbOverlay({ handGrip }: ThumbOverlayProps) {
  return (
    <div
      id="thumb-overlay"
      aria-hidden="true"
      data-hand={handGrip}
      className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 z-10 flex flex-col justify-between select-none overflow-hidden transition-all duration-300"
    >
      <div className="relative h-[26%] border-b border-dashed border-rose-500/40 bg-rose-500/[0.04] transition-all">
        <div className="absolute right-2 top-2">
          <span className="rounded-full bg-rose-500/15 border border-rose-500/30 px-2 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400">
            Difícil
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="relative flex-1 border-b border-dashed border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/[0.03] transition-all flex items-center justify-end pr-2">
        <span className="rounded-full bg-amber-500/15 border border-amber-500/30 px-2 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
          Esticada
        </span>
      </div>

      <div
        className={cn(
          "relative h-[38%] border-t-2 border-dashed border-primary/60 bg-primary/[0.06] transition-all duration-300 flex flex-col justify-end p-2",
          handGrip === "right" && "rounded-tl-[5rem] bg-gradient-to-tl from-primary/20 via-primary/8 to-transparent",
          handGrip === "left" && "rounded-tr-[5rem] bg-gradient-to-tr from-primary/20 via-primary/8 to-transparent",
          handGrip === "both" && "rounded-t-[3.5rem] bg-gradient-to-t from-primary/18 via-primary/8 to-transparent"
        )}
      >
        <div
          className={cn(
            "flex items-center",
            handGrip === "right" && "justify-end",
            handGrip === "left" && "justify-start",
            handGrip === "both" && "justify-center"
          )}
        >
          <span className="rounded-full bg-primary/20 border border-primary/40 px-2 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold text-primary shadow-2xs">
            {handGrip === "right" ? "Natural (Destro)" : handGrip === "left" ? "Natural (Canhoto)" : "Natural (2 Mãos)"}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import type {
  SimulatorSettings,
  TouchEventData,
  ThumbZone,
  ScreenScenario,
} from "./types";
import { HandSelector } from "./controls/hand-selector";
import { ZoneToggle } from "./controls/zone-toggle";
import { TargetCalibrator } from "./controls/target-calibrator";
import { PhoneFrame } from "./phone/phone-frame";
import { ThumbOverlay } from "./phone/thumb-overlay";
import { ScreenContent } from "./phone/screen-content";
import { TouchFeedback } from "./diagnostics/touch-feedback";
import { Reveal } from "@/components/reveal";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowLeft, Sliders, Smartphone } from "lucide-react";
import {
  Badge,
  SegmentedControl,
  SegmentedControlItem,
} from "@/components/ui";

export function PlaygroundView() {
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<SimulatorSettings>({
    handGrip: "right",
    showOverlay: true,
    targetSize: "48px",
    activeScreen: "feed",
  });

  const [lastTouch, setLastTouch] = useState<TouchEventData | null>(null);

  const handleElementClick = (elementClicked: string, zone: ThumbZone) => {
    setLastTouch({
      x: 0,
      y: 0,
      zone,
      elementClicked,
    });
  };

  const scenarios: { id: ScreenScenario; label: string; desc: string }[] = [
    { id: "feed", label: "Feed Notícias", desc: "Cards & leitura passiva" },
    { id: "form", label: "Formulário", desc: "Inputs & digitação" },
    { id: "actions", label: "Ações & FAB", desc: "Botões de alto alcance" },
  ];

  return (
    <section className="relative isolate overflow-clip border-b border-line/40">
      <div className="bg-dots absolute inset-0 -z-10 opacity-70 [mask-image:radial-gradient(ellipse_at_top,black,transparent_60%)]" />
      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-1/2 -z-10 h-72 w-full max-w-4xl -translate-x-1/2 bg-radial from-primary/15 via-transparent to-transparent opacity-60 blur-2xl" />

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 xl:px-12 pt-6 sm:pt-8 pb-20 lg:pb-24">
        <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-start">
          <Link
            href="/"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-line bg-surface/80 px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-muted backdrop-blur-sm transition-all hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink active:scale-95 shadow-2xs"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="size-3.5 text-primary" />
            <span>Voltar ao Início</span>
          </Link>
        </div>

        <header className="mx-auto max-w-3xl text-center mb-10 lg:mb-14">
          <Reveal>
            <Badge
              variant="outline"
              shape="pill"
              size="lg"
              dot
              pulse
              className="mb-3 backdrop-blur-xs font-semibold text-muted"
            >
              Módulo 03 • Laboratório Interativo
            </Badge>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-ink sm:text-4xl lg:text-5xl lg:leading-[1.15]">
              Playground{" "}
              <span className="text-primary underline decoration-primary/30 underline-offset-8">
                Ergonômico
              </span>
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-4 text-base leading-relaxed text-muted sm:text-lg">
              Simule a anatomia do toque, teste diferentes pegadas e avalie o impacto das zonas de alcance do polegar na prática.
            </p>
          </Reveal>
        </header>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 items-start">
          
          <div className="lg:col-span-4 xl:col-span-4 order-2 lg:order-1">
            <Reveal delay={0.05}>
              <div className="h-[610px] sm:h-[640px] w-full rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-5 shadow-xs flex flex-col justify-between select-none">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-line/50 pb-3">
                    <div className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-primary-light text-primary">
                      <Sliders className="size-4" />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-ink">Painel de Parâmetros</h2>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-muted">Ajuste as variáveis do teste</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="mt-4">
                    <HandSelector
                      value={settings.handGrip}
                      onChange={(handGrip) => setSettings((s) => ({ ...s, handGrip }))}
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="mt-4 space-y-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
                        <Smartphone className="size-3.5 text-primary" />
                        <span>Cenário da Tela</span>
                      </label>
                    </div>

                    <SegmentedControl
                      value={settings.activeScreen}
                      onValueChange={(val) =>
                        setSettings((s) => ({
                          ...s,
                          activeScreen: val as ScreenScenario,
                        }))
                      }
                      variant="raised"
                      itemVariant="surface"
                      fullWidth
                      className="grid grid-cols-3"
                    >
                      {scenarios.map((scen) => (
                        <SegmentedControlItem
                          key={scen.id}
                          value={scen.id}
                          detail={scen.desc.split(" ")[0]}
                          className="flex-col py-2"
                        >
                          {scen.label}
                        </SegmentedControlItem>
                      ))}
                    </SegmentedControl>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-4">
                  <ZoneToggle
                    checked={settings.showOverlay}
                    onChange={(showOverlay) => setSettings((s) => ({ ...s, showOverlay }))}
                  />

                  <TargetCalibrator
                    value={settings.targetSize}
                    onChange={(targetSize) => setSettings((s) => ({ ...s, targetSize }))}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </Reveal>
          </div>

          <div className="lg:col-span-4 xl:col-span-4 order-1 lg:order-2 flex items-center justify-center">
            <Reveal delay={0.1}>
              <PhoneFrame>
                <ScreenContent
                  scenario={settings.activeScreen}
                  targetSize={settings.targetSize}
                  onElementClick={handleElementClick}
                />

                {settings.showOverlay && (
                  <ThumbOverlay handGrip={settings.handGrip} />
                )}
              </PhoneFrame>
            </Reveal>
          </div>

          <div className="lg:col-span-4 xl:col-span-4 order-3 lg:order-3">
            <Reveal delay={0.15}>
              <TouchFeedback
                lastTouch={lastTouch}
                targetSize={settings.targetSize}
              />
            </Reveal>
          </div>

        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

"use client";

import { motion } from "motion/react";

export function Reveal({
    children,
    delay = 0,
    className,
}: {
    children: React.ReactNode;
    delay?: number;
    className?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      className={className}
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 28 }}
      whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      viewport={{ once: true, margin: "-80px" }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.6, delay, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
    >
      {children}
    </motion.div>
  );
}

================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/theme-toggle.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import { flushSync } from "react-dom";
import { Moon, Sun } from "lucide-react";
import { useTheme } from "next-themes";

export function ThemeToggle() {
  const { resolvedTheme, setTheme } = useTheme();

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    const nextTheme = resolvedTheme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark";
    const root = document.documentElement;

    const applyTheme = () => {
      root.classList.toggle("dark", nextTheme === "dark");
      root.style.colorScheme = nextTheme;
      flushSync(() => setTheme(nextTheme));
    };

    const reduceMotion = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

    if (!("startViewTransition" in document) || reduceMotion) {
      applyTheme();
      return;
    }

    root.classList.add("theme-switching");
    const transition = document.startViewTransition(applyTheme);
    transition.finished.finally(() => root.classList.remove("theme-switching"));
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      aria-label="Alternar tema claro/escuro"
      onClick={toggleTheme}
      className="group relative inline-flex size-11 md:size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface text-ink transition-all duration-300 hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-surface-raised active:scale-95 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary"
    >
      <div className="relative size-[18px]">
        <Sun className="absolute inset-0 size-[18px] text-ink rotate-0 scale-100 transition-all duration-300 ease-out dark:-rotate-90 dark:scale-0 dark:opacity-0" />
        <Moon className="absolute inset-0 size-[18px] text-primary rotate-90 scale-0 opacity-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-out dark:rotate-0 dark:scale-100 dark:opacity-100" />
      </div>
    </button>
  );
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/ui/badge.tsx
================================================================================

import * as React from "react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export const badgeVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center font-bold uppercase tracking-wider select-none border transition-colors",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        primary:
          "border-primary/30 bg-primary/10 text-primary",
        success:
          "border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600",
        info:
          "border-blue-500/30 bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600",
        warning:
          "border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600",
        danger:
          "border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-600",
        purple:
          "border-purple-500/30 bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-600",
        neutral:
          "border-line/60 bg-surface-raised text-muted",
        outline:
          "border-line bg-surface text-ink",
      },
      shape: {
        rounded: "rounded-md",
        pill: "rounded-full",
      },
      size: {
        sm: "text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.25 gap-1",
        md: "text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 gap-1.5",
        lg: "text-xs px-2.5 py-1 gap-2",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "primary",
      shape: "rounded",
      size: "md",
    },
  }
);

export interface BadgeProps
  extends React.ComponentProps<"span">,
    VariantProps<typeof badgeVariants> {
  icon?: React.ReactNode | React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
  dot?: boolean;
  pulse?: boolean;
}

export function Badge({
  className,
  variant = "primary",
  shape = "rounded",
  size = "md",
  icon,
  dot = false,
  pulse = false,
  children,
  ...props
}: BadgeProps) {
  const renderIcon = () => {
    if (!icon) return null;
    if (React.isValidElement(icon)) return icon;
    const IconComponent = icon as React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
    const iconSizeClass =
      size === "sm" ? "size-2.5" : size === "lg" ? "size-3.5" : "size-3";
    return <IconComponent className={cn(iconSizeClass, "shrink-0")} />;
  };

  return (
    <span
      className={cn(badgeVariants({ variant, shape, size }), className)}
      {...props}
    >
      {dot && (
        <span className="relative flex size-1.5 shrink-0">
          {pulse && (
            <span className="absolute inline-flex h-full w-full animate-ping rounded-full bg-current opacity-75" />
          )}
          <span className="relative inline-flex size-1.5 rounded-full bg-current" />
        </span>
      )}

      {renderIcon()}

      {children && <span>{children}</span>}
    </span>
  );
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/ui/bottom-nav.tsx
================================================================================

import * as React from "react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export const bottomNavVariants = cva(
  "relative flex items-center transition-all duration-200 select-none",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        standard:
          "h-14 w-full justify-around border-t border-line/70 bg-surface-raised/80 px-1 backdrop-blur-sm",
        "with-fab":
          "relative h-14 w-full justify-around border-t border-line/70 bg-surface-raised/80 px-1 backdrop-blur-sm",
        floating:
          "h-12 w-full max-w-[280px] justify-around rounded-full border border-line bg-surface/90 px-2 shadow-md backdrop-blur-md",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "standard",
    },
  }
);

export type BottomNavVariant = NonNullable<
  VariantProps<typeof bottomNavVariants>["variant"]
>;

interface BottomNavContextValue {
  variant: BottomNavVariant;
}

const BottomNavContext = React.createContext<BottomNavContextValue>({
  variant: "standard",
});

export interface BottomNavProps
  extends React.ComponentProps<"nav">,
    VariantProps<typeof bottomNavVariants> {}

export function BottomNav({
  className,
  variant = "standard",
  children,
  ...props
}: BottomNavProps) {
  const resolvedVariant = variant ?? "standard";

  return (
    <BottomNavContext.Provider value={{ variant: resolvedVariant }}>
      <nav
        role="navigation"
        className={cn(bottomNavVariants({ variant: resolvedVariant }), className)}
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
      </nav>
    </BottomNavContext.Provider>
  );
}

export interface BottomNavItemProps extends React.ComponentProps<"button"> {
  icon?: React.ReactNode | React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
  label?: string;
  active?: boolean;
  badge?: number | string | boolean | React.ReactNode;
  variant?: BottomNavVariant;
}

export function BottomNavItem({
  icon,
  label,
  active = false,
  badge,
  variant: itemVariant,
  className,
  type = "button",
  title,
  children,
  ...props
}: BottomNavItemProps) {
  const context = React.useContext(BottomNavContext);
  const variant = itemVariant ?? context.variant;
  const isFloating = variant === "floating";

  const renderIcon = () => {
    if (!icon) return null;
    if (React.isValidElement(icon)) {
      return icon;
    }
    const IconComponent = icon as React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
    return (
      <IconComponent
        className={cn(
          isFloating ? "size-4" : "size-4.5",
          "transition-transform",
          active && !isFloating && "scale-110"
        )}
      />
    );
  };

  if (isFloating) {
    return (
      <button
        type={type}
        aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
        title={title ?? label}
        className={cn(
          "flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full transition-all active:scale-85 cursor-pointer",
          active
            ? "bg-primary text-on-primary shadow-xs"
            : "text-muted hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink",
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {renderIcon()}
        {children}
      </button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <button
      type={type}
      aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
      title={title ?? label}
      className={cn(
        "relative flex min-h-[44px] min-w-[48px] flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl py-1 transition-transform active:scale-90 cursor-pointer",
        active ? "text-primary font-semibold" : "text-muted hover:text-ink",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      <div className="relative">
        {renderIcon()}
        {badge !== undefined && badge !== null && badge !== false && (
          <span className="absolute -right-2 -top-1 flex size-3.5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary text-[8px] font-bold text-on-primary">
            {badge === true ? "" : badge}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      {label && <span className="text-[10px] tracking-tight">{label}</span>}

      {active && (
        <span className="absolute -top-[1px] h-0.5 w-6 rounded-full bg-primary" />
      )}
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

export interface BottomNavFabProps extends React.ComponentProps<"button"> {
  icon?: React.ReactNode | React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
  label?: string;
  badge?: number | string | boolean | React.ReactNode;
}

export function BottomNavFab({
  icon,
  label = "Criar",
  badge,
  className,
  type = "button",
  title = "Ação Primária Central",
  children,
  ...props
}: BottomNavFabProps) {
  const renderIcon = () => {
    if (!icon) return null;
    if (React.isValidElement(icon)) {
      return icon;
    }
    const IconComponent = icon as React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
    return <IconComponent className="size-5 stroke-[2.5]" />;
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative -mt-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
      <button
        type={type}
        title={title}
        aria-label={props["aria-label"] ?? label}
        className={cn(
          "relative flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary text-on-primary shadow-md transition-all hover:scale-105 active:scale-90 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary cursor-pointer",
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {renderIcon()}
        {badge !== undefined && badge !== null && badge !== false && (
          <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 flex size-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-surface text-[9px] font-bold text-primary shadow-xs">
            {badge === true ? "" : badge}
          </span>
        )}
        {children}
      </button>
      {label && (
        <span className="mt-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold text-primary">
          {label}
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";

export const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl font-medium transition-all duration-200 active:scale-[0.97] focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-primary disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        primary:
          "bg-primary text-on-primary shadow-sm hover:bg-primary-hover hover:shadow-md",

          "bg-surface-raised text-ink border border-line hover:border-primary/50",
