================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/ui/card.tsx
================================================================================

import * as React from "react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export const cardVariants = cva(
  "rounded-2xl border transition-all duration-200",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "border-line bg-surface text-ink shadow-2xs",
        interactive:
          "group cursor-pointer border-line bg-surface text-ink shadow-2xs hover:border-primary/50 hover:shadow-md active:scale-[0.98] focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary",
        raised:
          "border-line/80 bg-surface-raised text-ink shadow-sm hover:shadow-md",
        outline:
          "border-line bg-surface/80 text-ink backdrop-blur-sm",
        ghost:
          "border-transparent bg-transparent text-ink hover:bg-surface-raised/60",
      },
      padding: {
        none: "p-0",
        sm: "p-3",
        md: "p-4",
        lg: "p-6",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      padding: "md",
    },
  }
);

export interface CardProps
  extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement>,
    VariantProps<typeof cardVariants> {
  as?: "article" | "div" | "section";
}

export function Card({
  as: Component = "article",
  className,
  variant,
  padding,
  onClick,
  ...props
}: CardProps) {
  const computedVariant = variant ?? (onClick ? "interactive" : "default");

  return (
    <Component
      onClick={onClick}
      className={cn(
        cardVariants({ variant: computedVariant, padding }),
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export function CardHeader({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return <div className={cn("flex flex-col gap-1.5", className)} {...props} />;
}

export function CardTitle({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"h3">) {
  return (
    <h3
      className={cn(
        "text-base font-bold text-ink leading-snug transition-colors group-hover:text-primary",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export function CardDescription({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"p">) {
  return (
    <p
      className={cn("text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export function CardContent({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return <div className={cn("flex-1", className)} {...props} />;
}

export function CardFooter({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "flex items-center justify-between border-t border-line/40 pt-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-primary",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export interface CardActionProps extends React.ComponentProps<"button"> {
  size?: "md" | "lg";
}

/**
 * CardAction isola ações secundárias dentro de um Card interativo.
 * Aplica stopPropagation automático e área de toque calibrada (44px/48px).
 */
export function CardAction({
  onClick,
  className,
  size = "md",
  ...props
}: CardActionProps) {
  const handleClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    onClick?.(e);
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={handleClick}
      className={cn(
        "flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl transition-all active:scale-90 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary",
        size === "md" ? "size-11" : "size-12",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/ui/checkbox.tsx
================================================================================

import * as React from "react";
import { Check, Minus } from "lucide-react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export const checkboxBoxVariants = cva(
  "flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center border transition-all duration-200 select-none",
  {
    variants: {
      size: {
        md: "size-5 rounded-md", // 20px
        lg: "size-6 rounded-lg", // 24px (Padrão ouro Hapto)
      },
      state: {
        unchecked: "border-line bg-surface-raised/40 hover:border-primary/50",
        checked: "border-primary bg-primary text-on-primary shadow-xs",
        indeterminate: "border-primary bg-primary text-on-primary shadow-xs",
        error: "border-rose-500 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      size: "lg",
      state: "unchecked",
    },
  }
);

export interface CheckboxProps
  extends Omit<React.ComponentProps<"input">, "size" | "onChange">,
    VariantProps<typeof checkboxBoxVariants> {
  checked?: boolean;
  defaultChecked?: boolean;
  onCheckedChange?: (checked: boolean) => void;
  onChange?: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void;
  indeterminate?: boolean;
  label?: React.ReactNode;
  description?: React.ReactNode;
  error?: string;
  variant?: "default" | "card";
}

export function Checkbox({
  checked,
  defaultChecked,
  onCheckedChange,
  onChange,
  indeterminate = false,
  label,
  description,
  error,
  variant = "default",
  size = "lg",
  disabled = false,
  className,
  id,
  ...props
}: CheckboxProps) {
  const generatedId = React.useId();
  const checkboxId = id ?? generatedId;
  const descriptionId = `${checkboxId}-desc`;
  const errorId = `${checkboxId}-error`;

  const [internalChecked, setInternalChecked] = React.useState(
    defaultChecked ?? false
  );

  const isChecked = checked !== undefined ? checked : internalChecked;

  const handleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (disabled) return;
    if (checked === undefined) {
      setInternalChecked(e.target.checked);
    }
    onCheckedChange?.(e.target.checked);
    onChange?.(e);
  };

  const computedState = error
    ? "error"
    : indeterminate
    ? "indeterminate"
    : isChecked
    ? "checked"
    : "unchecked";

  const boxSizeClass = size === "md" ? "size-3.5 stroke-[2.5]" : "size-4 stroke-[2.5]";

  const touchTargetWrapperClass =
    size === "md"
      ? "relative inline-flex min-h-11 min-w-11 items-center justify-center"
      : "relative inline-flex min-h-12 min-w-12 items-center justify-center";

  const checkboxControl = (
    <div className={touchTargetWrapperClass}>
      <input
        id={checkboxId}
        type="checkbox"
        checked={isChecked}
        disabled={disabled}
        onChange={handleChange}
        aria-checked={indeterminate ? "mixed" : isChecked}
        aria-invalid={!!error}
        aria-describedby={
          error ? errorId : description ? descriptionId : undefined
        }
        className="peer sr-only"
        {...props}
      />
      <div
        className={cn(
          checkboxBoxVariants({ size, state: computedState }),
          "peer-focus-visible:ring-2 peer-focus-visible:ring-primary peer-focus-visible:ring-offset-2 peer-focus-visible:ring-offset-surface",
          disabled && "cursor-not-allowed opacity-50",
          className
        )}
      >
        {indeterminate ? (
          <Minus className={boxSizeClass} />
        ) : isChecked ? (
          <Check className={boxSizeClass} />
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  if (!label && !description && !error) {
    return checkboxControl;
  }

  const containerVariants = {
    default: cn(
      "flex min-h-12 items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer select-none rounded-xl p-1 transition-colors hover:bg-surface-raised/50",
      disabled && "cursor-not-allowed opacity-50"
    ),
    card: cn(
      "flex min-h-12 w-full cursor-pointer select-none items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface px-4 py-2.5 shadow-2xs transition-all hover:border-primary/50 active:scale-[0.99]",
      disabled && "cursor-not-allowed opacity-50"
    ),
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full space-y-1">
      <label htmlFor={checkboxId} className={containerVariants[variant]}>
        {/* Checkbox box control - no container padding when in label/card */}
        <div className="relative flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center">
          <input
            id={checkboxId}
            type="checkbox"
            checked={isChecked}
            disabled={disabled}
            onChange={handleChange}
            aria-checked={indeterminate ? "mixed" : isChecked}
            aria-invalid={!!error}
            aria-describedby={
              error ? errorId : description ? descriptionId : undefined
            }
            className="peer sr-only"
            {...props}
          />
          <div
            className={cn(
              checkboxBoxVariants({ size, state: computedState }),
              "peer-focus-visible:ring-2 peer-focus-visible:ring-primary peer-focus-visible:ring-offset-2 peer-focus-visible:ring-offset-surface",
              className
            )}
          >
            {indeterminate ? (
              <Minus className={boxSizeClass} />
            ) : isChecked ? (
              <Check className={boxSizeClass} />
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col">
          {label && (
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink leading-snug">
              {label}
            </span>
          )}
          {description && (
            <span
              id={descriptionId}
              className="text-[10px] text-muted leading-relaxed"
            >
              {description}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </label>

      {error && (
        <p
          id={errorId}
          role="alert"
          className="text-xs font-medium text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 pl-1"
        >
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/ui/chip.tsx
================================================================================

import * as React from "react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export const chipVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-all duration-200 select-none cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.97] focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-primary disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        outline:
          "border border-line bg-surface text-muted hover:border-primary/50 hover:text-ink",
        filled:
          "border border-line/60 bg-surface-raised text-ink hover:border-primary/40",
        subtle:
          "border border-primary/30 bg-primary-light text-primary",
        primary:
          "border-primary bg-primary text-on-primary shadow-2xs hover:bg-primary-hover",
      },
      size: {
        // sm: 28px de altura (sugestões rápidas e tags compactas)
        sm: "min-h-7 px-2.5 text-[11px] rounded-lg",
        // md: 36px de altura (filtros intermediários)
        md: "min-h-9 px-3 text-xs rounded-xl",
        // lg: 44px de altura (Padrão mínimo Apple HIG / Hapto Mobile para polegar)
        lg: "min-h-11 px-4 text-sm rounded-xl",
      },
      selected: {
        true: "",
        false: "",
      },
    },
    compoundVariants: [
      {
        selected: true,
        variant: "outline",
        className: "border-primary bg-primary-light text-primary font-semibold shadow-2xs",
      },
      {
        selected: true,
        variant: "filled",
        className: "border-primary bg-primary text-on-primary font-semibold shadow-2xs",
      },
    ],
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "outline",
      size: "lg",
      selected: false,
    },
  }
);

export interface ChipProps
  extends React.ComponentProps<"button">,
    Omit<VariantProps<typeof chipVariants>, "selected"> {
  selected?: boolean;
  active?: boolean;
  icon?: React.ReactNode | React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
  onDismiss?: () => void;
  dismissLabel?: string;
}

export function Chip({
  className,
  variant = "outline",
  size = "lg",
  selected,
  active,
  icon,
  onDismiss,
  dismissLabel = "Remover filtro",
  type = "button",
  children,
  ...props
}: ChipProps) {
  const isSelected = selected ?? active ?? false;

  const renderIcon = () => {
    if (!icon) return null;
    if (React.isValidElement(icon)) return icon;
    const IconComponent = icon as React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
    const iconSizeClass =
      size === "sm" ? "size-3" : size === "md" ? "size-3.5" : "size-4";
    return <IconComponent className={cn(iconSizeClass, "shrink-0")} />;
  };

  return (
    <button
      type={type}
      aria-pressed={isSelected}
      className={cn(
        chipVariants({ variant, size, selected: isSelected }),
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {renderIcon()}

      <span>{children}</span>

      {onDismiss && (
        <span
          role="button"
          tabIndex={0}
          aria-label={dismissLabel}
          onClick={(e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            onDismiss();
          }}
          onKeyDown={(e) => {
            if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
              e.preventDefault();
              e.stopPropagation();
              onDismiss();
            }
          }}
          className="ml-0.5 -mr-1 flex size-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full opacity-70 hover:opacity-100 hover:bg-black/10 dark:hover:bg-white/10 active:scale-90 transition-opacity"
        >
          <X className="size-3" />
        </span>
      )}
    </button>
  );
}

// Alias FilterChip para semântica de filtros
export const FilterChip = Chip;


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/ui/fab.tsx
================================================================================

import * as React from "react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export const fabVariants = cva(
  "relative inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center font-semibold transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-primary disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 select-none cursor-pointer",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        primary:
          "bg-primary text-on-primary shadow-md shadow-primary/25 hover:bg-primary-hover hover:scale-105 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-primary/30",
        secondary:
          "border border-line bg-surface-raised text-ink shadow-md hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-surface hover:scale-105",
        surface:
          "border border-line bg-surface text-ink shadow-md hover:border-primary/50 hover:scale-105",
        contrast:
          "bg-ink text-surface shadow-md hover:opacity-90 hover:scale-105",
      },
      size: {
        // sm: 40px (Mini FAB para espaços compactos)
        sm: "size-10 text-xs",
        // md: 48px (Padrão ouro Hapto & WCAG)
        md: "size-12 text-sm",
        // lg: 56px (Padrão Ouro FAB para polegar)
        lg: "size-14 text-base",
      },
      extended: {
        true: "w-auto px-5 rounded-full gap-2",
        false: "rounded-full",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "primary",
      size: "lg",
      extended: false,
    },
  }
);

export type FabPosition =
  | "bottom-right"
  | "bottom-left"
  | "bottom-center"
  | "none";

const POSITION_CLASSES: Record<FabPosition, string> = {
  "bottom-right": "fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-40",
  "bottom-left": "fixed bottom-6 left-6 z-40",
  "bottom-center": "fixed bottom-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40",
  none: "",
};

export interface FabProps
  extends React.ComponentProps<"button">,
    Omit<VariantProps<typeof fabVariants>, "extended"> {
  extended?: boolean;
  position?: FabPosition;
  icon?: React.ReactNode | React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
  label?: string;
  badge?: boolean | React.ReactNode;
}

export function Fab({
  className,
  variant = "primary",
  size = "lg",
  extended = false,
  position = "none",
  icon,
  label,
  badge,
  type = "button",
  children,
  ...props
}: FabProps) {
  const renderIcon = () => {
    if (!icon) return null;
    if (React.isValidElement(icon)) return icon;
    const IconComponent = icon as React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
    const iconSizeClass =
      size === "sm" ? "size-4" : size === "md" ? "size-5" : "size-6 stroke-[2.5]";
    return <IconComponent className={iconSizeClass} />;
  };

  const positionClass = POSITION_CLASSES[position];

  return (
    <button
      type={type}
      aria-label={label ?? (typeof children === "string" ? children : "Ação rápida")}
      className={cn(
        fabVariants({ variant, size, extended }),
        positionClass,
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {renderIcon()}

      {extended && label && <span>{label}</span>}

      {children}

      {!extended && label && !children && (
        <span className="sr-only">{label}</span>
      )}

      {badge === true && (
        <span className="absolute top-1.5 right-1.5 flex size-2.5 pointer-events-none">
          <span className="absolute inline-flex h-full w-full animate-ping rounded-full bg-primary opacity-75" />
          <span className="relative inline-flex size-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary shadow-2xs" />
        </span>
      )}

      {(typeof badge === "number" || typeof badge === "string") && (
        <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 flex min-w-5 h-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary px-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-on-primary shadow-xs pointer-events-none">
          {badge}
        </span>
      )}

      {typeof badge === "object" && badge !== null && (
        <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 pointer-events-none">
          {badge}
        </span>
      )}
    </button>
  );
}

// Alias FAB para conveniência
export const FAB = Fab;


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/ui/feedback.tsx
================================================================================

import * as React from "react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export const feedbackVariants = cva(
  "transition-all duration-200 select-none",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        snackbar:
          "flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface-raised/95 p-2 shadow-sm backdrop-blur-sm",
        banner:
          "flex w-full flex-col gap-1 rounded-xl border p-2 shadow-xs",
        floating:
          "flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-line bg-surface/95 px-3 py-1.5 shadow-md backdrop-blur-sm",
      },
      status: {
        default: "",
        success: "",
        warning: "",
        error: "",
        info: "",
      },
      fixed: {
        true: "fixed bottom-4 left-4 right-4 z-50 mx-auto max-w-md animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-2",
        false: "",
      },
    },
    compoundVariants: [
      {
        variant: "banner",
        status: "default",
        className: "border-line bg-surface text-ink",
      },
      {
        variant: "banner",
        status: "warning",
        className: "border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-950 dark:text-amber-100",
      },
      {
        variant: "banner",
        status: "success",
        className: "border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-100",
      },
      {
        variant: "banner",
        status: "error",
        className: "border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 text-red-950 dark:text-red-100",
      },
      {
        variant: "banner",
        status: "info",
        className: "border-blue-500/30 bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-950 dark:text-blue-100",
      },
    ],
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "snackbar",
      status: "default",
      fixed: false,
    },
  }
);

export type FeedbackVariant = NonNullable<
  VariantProps<typeof feedbackVariants>["variant"]
>;

export type FeedbackStatus = NonNullable<
  VariantProps<typeof feedbackVariants>["status"]
>;

interface FeedbackContextValue {
  variant: FeedbackVariant;
  status: FeedbackStatus;
}

const FeedbackContext = React.createContext<FeedbackContextValue>({
  variant: "snackbar",
  status: "default",
});

export interface FeedbackProps
  extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>,
    VariantProps<typeof feedbackVariants> {
  as?: "div" | "aside" | "section";
}

export function Feedback({
  className,
  variant = "snackbar",
  status = "default",
  fixed = false,
  as: Component = "div",
  role,
  "aria-live": ariaLive,
  children,
  ...props
}: FeedbackProps) {
  const resolvedVariant = variant ?? "snackbar";
  const resolvedStatus = status ?? "default";

  const defaultRole =
    role ?? (resolvedVariant === "banner" || resolvedStatus === "warning" || resolvedStatus === "error"
      ? "alert"
      : "status");

  const defaultAriaLive =
    ariaLive ?? (defaultRole === "alert" ? "assertive" : "polite");

  return (
    <FeedbackContext.Provider
      value={{ variant: resolvedVariant, status: resolvedStatus }}
    >
      <Component
        role={defaultRole}
        aria-live={defaultAriaLive}
        className={cn(
          feedbackVariants({
            variant: resolvedVariant,
            status: resolvedStatus,
            fixed,
          }),
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
      </Component>
    </FeedbackContext.Provider>
  );
}

export interface FeedbackIconProps extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLSpanElement> {
  icon?: React.ReactNode | React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
}

export function FeedbackIcon({
  icon,
  className,
  children,
  ...props
}: FeedbackIconProps) {
  const { variant, status } = React.useContext(FeedbackContext);

  const getStatusColor = () => {
    switch (status) {
      case "success":
        return "text-emerald-500";
      case "warning":
        return "text-amber-600";
      case "error":
        return "text-red-500";
      case "info":
        return "text-blue-500";
      default:
        return variant === "floating" ? "text-primary" : "text-ink";
    }
  };

  const iconSizeClass = variant === "snackbar" ? "size-4" : "size-3.5";

  const renderIcon = () => {
    if (!icon) return null;
    if (React.isValidElement(icon)) {
      return icon;
    }
    const IconComponent = icon as React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
    return <IconComponent className={cn(iconSizeClass, "shrink-0", getStatusColor())} />;
  };

  return (
    <span
      className={cn("inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center", className)}
      {...props}
    >
      {renderIcon()}
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

export interface FeedbackTitleProps extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement> {
  as?: "span" | "h4" | "p" | "div";
}

export function FeedbackTitle({
  as: Component = "span",
  className,
  ...props
}: FeedbackTitleProps) {
  const { variant } = React.useContext(FeedbackContext);

  const variantStyles = {
    snackbar: "truncate text-[11px] font-medium text-ink",
    banner: "truncate text-[11px] font-bold text-ink",
    floating: "text-[11px] font-semibold text-ink",
  }[variant];

  return <Component className={cn(variantStyles, className)} {...props} />;
}

export interface FeedbackDescriptionProps
  extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLParagraphElement> {
  as?: "p" | "span";
}

export function FeedbackDescription({
  as: Component = "span",
  className,
  ...props
}: FeedbackDescriptionProps) {
  return (
    <Component
      className={cn("text-[10px] text-muted", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export type FeedbackActionsProps = React.ComponentProps<"div">;

export function FeedbackActions({
  className,
  ...props
}: FeedbackActionsProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn("flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export const feedbackActionVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer select-none focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        primary:
          "h-7 gap-1 rounded-lg px-2 text-[11px] text-primary hover:bg-primary/10 active:scale-90",
        secondary:
          "rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] active:scale-90",
        ghost:
          "rounded-lg p-1 text-muted hover:bg-surface hover:text-ink active:scale-90",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "primary",
    },
  }
);

export interface FeedbackActionProps
  extends React.ComponentProps<"button">,
    VariantProps<typeof feedbackActionVariants> {
  icon?: React.ReactNode | React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
}

export function FeedbackAction({
  variant,
  icon,
  className,
  children,
  type = "button",
  ...props
}: FeedbackActionProps) {
  const { variant: containerVariant, status } = React.useContext(FeedbackContext);
  const resolvedVariant = variant ?? (containerVariant === "banner" ? "secondary" : "primary");

  const getSecondaryStatusClasses = () => {
    switch (status) {
      case "warning":
        return "bg-amber-600/20 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 hover:bg-amber-600/30";
      case "success":
        return "bg-emerald-600/20 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 hover:bg-emerald-600/30";
      case "error":
        return "bg-red-600/20 text-red-700 dark:text-red-300 hover:bg-red-600/30";
      case "info":
        return "bg-blue-600/20 text-blue-700 dark:text-blue-300 hover:bg-blue-600/30";
      default:
        return "bg-surface-raised text-ink hover:bg-surface-raised/80";
    }
  };

  const renderIcon = () => {
    if (!icon) return null;
    if (React.isValidElement(icon)) {
      return icon;
    }
    const IconComponent = icon as React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
    return <IconComponent className="size-3 shrink-0" />;
  };

  return (
    <button
      type={type}
      className={cn(
        feedbackActionVariants({ variant: resolvedVariant }),
        resolvedVariant === "secondary" && getSecondaryStatusClasses(),
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {renderIcon()}
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

export interface FeedbackCloseProps
  extends Omit<React.ComponentProps<"button">, "children"> {
  icon?: React.ReactNode | React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
}

export function FeedbackClose({
  className,
  icon,
  "aria-label": ariaLabel = "Dispensar notificação",
  type = "button",
  ...props
}: FeedbackCloseProps) {
  const { variant } = React.useContext(FeedbackContext);

  const containerClasses = {
    snackbar:
      "flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-muted transition-colors hover:bg-surface hover:text-ink active:scale-90 cursor-pointer",
    banner:
      "flex size-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded text-muted transition-colors hover:text-ink active:scale-90 cursor-pointer",
    floating:
      "ml-1 flex size-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-muted hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink active:scale-80 cursor-pointer",
  }[variant];

  const iconSizeClass = variant === "snackbar" ? "size-3.5" : "size-3";

  return (
    <button
      type={type}
      aria-label={ariaLabel}
      className={cn(containerClasses, className)}
      {...props}
    >
      {icon ? (
        React.isValidElement(icon) ? (
          icon
        ) : (
          React.createElement(icon as React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>, {
            className: iconSizeClass,
          })
        )
      ) : (
        <X className={iconSizeClass} />
      )}
    </button>
  );
}

// Preset aliases
export function Snackbar(props: FeedbackProps) {
  return <Feedback variant="snackbar" {...props} />;
}

export function AlertBanner(props: FeedbackProps) {
  return <Feedback variant="banner" role="alert" {...props} />;
}

export function Toast(props: FeedbackProps) {
  return <Feedback variant="floating" {...props} />;
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/ui/icon-button.tsx
================================================================================

import * as React from "react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export const iconButtonVariants = cva(
  "relative inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl transition-all duration-200 active:scale-90 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary focus-visible:outline-offset-2 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 select-none cursor-pointer",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          "border border-line bg-surface text-muted hover:border-primary/50 hover:text-ink shadow-2xs",
        outline:
          "border border-line bg-surface text-ink hover:border-primary hover:text-primary shadow-2xs",
        ghost:
          "text-muted hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink",
        primary:
          "bg-primary text-on-primary hover:bg-primary-hover shadow-xs",
        secondary:
          "border border-line bg-surface-raised text-ink hover:border-primary/50",
      },
      size: {
        // sm: 40px (Espaços compactos)
        sm: "size-10",
        // md: 44px (Padrão mínimo Apple HIG)
        md: "size-11",
        // lg: 48px (Padrão ouro Hapto & WCAG)
        lg: "size-12",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "lg",
    },
  }
);

export interface IconButtonProps
  extends React.ComponentProps<"button">,
    VariantProps<typeof iconButtonVariants> {
  badge?: boolean | React.ReactNode;
  badgePing?: boolean;
}

export function IconButton({
  className,
  variant,
  size = "lg",
  badge,
  badgePing = true,
  type = "button",
  children,
  ...props
}: IconButtonProps) {
  return (
    <button
      type={type}
      className={cn(iconButtonVariants({ variant, size }), className)}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}

      {badge === true && (
        <span className="absolute top-2.5 right-2.5 flex size-2.5 pointer-events-none">
          {badgePing && (
            <span className="absolute inline-flex h-full w-full animate-ping rounded-full bg-primary opacity-75" />
          )}
          <span className="relative inline-flex size-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary shadow-2xs" />
        </span>
      )}

      {typeof badge === "number" || typeof badge === "string" ? (
        <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 flex min-w-5 h-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary px-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-on-primary shadow-xs pointer-events-none">
          {badge}
        </span>
      ) : typeof badge === "object" && badge !== null ? (
        <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 pointer-events-none">
          {badge}
        </span>
      ) : null}
    </button>
  );
}

ARQUIVO: components/ui/input.tsx
================================================================================

import * as React from "react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export const inputVariants = cva(
  "flex w-full rounded-xl border bg-surface text-ink transition-all duration-200 placeholder:text-muted/60 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 focus-visible:outline-none",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          "border-line hover:border-primary/50 focus-visible:border-primary focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary/20",
        error:
          "border-rose-500/80 text-rose-950 dark:text-rose-200 focus-visible:border-rose-500 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-rose-500/20",
        success:
          "border-emerald-500/80 text-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-200 focus-visible:border-emerald-500 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-emerald-500/20",
      },
      inputSize: {
        // md: 44px (Padrão mínimo Apple HIG)
        md: "min-h-11 px-3.5 text-base",
        // lg: 48px (Padrão ouro Hapto & WCAG - previne auto-zoom no iOS)
        lg: "min-h-12 px-4 text-base",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      inputSize: "lg",
    },
  }
);

export interface InputProps
  extends Omit<React.ComponentProps<"input">, "size">,
    VariantProps<typeof inputVariants> {
  startIcon?: React.ReactNode;
  endIcon?: React.ReactNode;
  error?: string;
  helperText?: string;
}

export function Input({
  className,
  variant,
  inputSize,
  type = "text",
  startIcon,
  endIcon,
  error,
  helperText,
  id,
  ...props
}: InputProps) {
  const generatedId = React.useId();
  const inputId = id ?? generatedId;
  const errorId = `${inputId}-error`;
  const helperId = `${inputId}-helper`;

  const computedVariant = error ? "error" : variant;

  return (
    <div className="w-full space-y-1.5">
      <div className="relative flex items-center">
        {startIcon && (
          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 flex items-center justify-center text-muted">
            {startIcon}
          </div>
        )}

        <input
          id={inputId}
          type={type}
          aria-invalid={!!error}
          aria-describedby={
            error ? errorId : helperText ? helperId : undefined
          }
          className={cn(
            inputVariants({ variant: computedVariant, inputSize }),
            startIcon && "pl-11",
            endIcon && "pr-11",
            className
          )}
          {...props}
        />

        {endIcon && (
          <div className="absolute right-3.5 flex items-center justify-center text-muted">
            {endIcon}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {error ? (
        <p
          id={errorId}
          role="alert"
          className="text-xs font-medium text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400"
        >
          {error}
        </p>
      ) : helperText ? (
        <p id={helperId} className="text-xs text-muted">
          {helperText}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/ui/search-input.tsx
================================================================================

import * as React from "react";
import { Search, X } from "lucide-react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export const searchInputVariants = cva(
  "relative flex w-full items-center rounded-xl border transition-all duration-200 shadow-2xs focus-within:border-primary focus-within:ring-2 focus-within:ring-primary/20",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "border-line bg-surface hover:border-primary/40",
        glass:
          "border-line bg-surface/90 backdrop-blur-md hover:border-primary/40",
      },
      searchSize: {
        // md: 44px (Padrão mínimo Apple HIG)
        md: "min-h-11 px-3 text-base sm:text-sm",
        // lg: 48px (Padrão ouro Hapto & WCAG - previne auto-zoom no iOS)
        lg: "min-h-12 px-3.5 text-base sm:text-sm",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      searchSize: "lg",
    },
  }
);

export interface SearchInputProps
  extends Omit<React.ComponentProps<"input">, "size">,
    VariantProps<typeof searchInputVariants> {
  value?: string;
  onClear?: () => void;
  startIcon?: React.ReactNode;
  endAdornment?: React.ReactNode;
  endAction?: React.ReactNode;
  inputRef?: React.Ref<HTMLInputElement>;
}

export function SearchInput({
  className,
  variant,
  searchSize = "lg",
  value,
  defaultValue = "",
  onChange,
  onClear,
  startIcon,
  endAdornment,
  endAction,
  inputRef,
  placeholder = "Buscar...",
  disabled = false,
  id,
  ...props
}: SearchInputProps) {
  const generatedId = React.useId();
  const searchId = id ?? generatedId;
  const internalInputRef = React.useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const actualRef = (inputRef as React.RefObject<HTMLInputElement>) || internalInputRef;

  const [internalValue, setInternalValue] = React.useState(
    defaultValue as string
  );
  const isControlled = value !== undefined;
  const currentValue = isControlled ? value : internalValue;
  const hasValue = Boolean(currentValue && String(currentValue).length > 0);

  const handleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (!isControlled) {
      setInternalValue(e.target.value);
    }
    onChange?.(e);
  };

  const handleClear = () => {
    if (!isControlled) {
      setInternalValue("");
    }
    onClear?.();

    if (actualRef && "current" in actualRef && actualRef.current) {
      actualRef.current.focus();

      // Dispara evento sintético caso esteja sob controle de formulário
      const nativeInputValueSetter = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(
        window.HTMLInputElement.prototype,
        "value"
      )?.set;
      if (nativeInputValueSetter) {
        nativeInputValueSetter.call(actualRef.current, "");
        const ev = new Event("input", { bubbles: true });
        actualRef.current.dispatchEvent(ev);
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        searchInputVariants({ variant, searchSize }),
        disabled && "opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed",
        className
      )}
    >
      <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center text-muted transition-colors focus-within:text-primary">
        {startIcon ?? <Search className="size-5 shrink-0" />}
      </div>

      <input
        ref={actualRef}
        id={searchId}
        type="search"
        value={isControlled ? value : internalValue}
        onChange={handleChange}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        disabled={disabled}
        aria-label={props["aria-label"] ?? placeholder}
        className="w-full bg-transparent px-3 text-base sm:text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-muted/70 focus:outline-none disabled:cursor-not-allowed [appearance:none] [&::-webkit-search-cancel-button]:hidden"
        {...props}
      />

      <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5">
        {endAdornment}

        {hasValue && !disabled && (
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Limpar campo de busca"
            onClick={handleClear}
            className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-muted transition-colors hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink active:scale-90 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary cursor-pointer"
            title="Limpar busca"
          >
            <X className="size-4" />
          </button>
        )}

        {endAction}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/ui/segmented-control.tsx
================================================================================

import * as React from "react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export const segmentedControlVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center border border-line bg-surface select-none shadow-xs transition-colors",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "border-line bg-surface",
        raised: "border-line/80 bg-surface-raised/70",
        outline: "border-line bg-transparent",
      },
      shape: {
        rounded: "rounded-xl p-1 gap-1",
        pill: "rounded-full p-1 gap-1",
      },
      size: {
        // sm: 32px de altura (densidade alta / espaço reduzido)
        sm: "min-h-8 p-0.5 gap-0.5 text-xs",
        // md: 40px de altura (padrão desktop / cards)
        md: "min-h-10 p-1 gap-1 text-xs",
        // lg: 48px de altura (Padrão Ouro Hapto & WCAG - alvo de toque do polegar)
        lg: "min-h-12 p-1 gap-1.5 text-sm",
      },
      fullWidth: {
        true: "w-full flex",
        false: "w-auto inline-flex",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      shape: "rounded",
      size: "md",
      fullWidth: false,
    },
  }
);

export const segmentedItemVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center font-semibold transition-all duration-200 select-none cursor-pointer active:scale-95 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-primary disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        primary: "",
        surface: "",
        subtle: "",
      },
      shape: {
        rounded: "rounded-lg",
        pill: "rounded-full",
      },
      size: {
        sm: "min-h-7 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs gap-1",
        md: "min-h-8 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs gap-1.5",
        lg: "min-h-10 px-4 py-2 text-sm gap-2",
      },
      selected: {
        true: "",
        false: "",
      },
    },
    compoundVariants: [
      {
        selected: true,
        variant: "primary",
        className:
          "bg-primary text-on-primary shadow-xs font-bold",
      },
      {
        selected: false,
        variant: "primary",
        className:
          "text-muted hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink",
      },
      {
        selected: true,
        variant: "surface",
        className:
          "bg-surface text-ink font-bold shadow-xs border border-line/80 ring-1 ring-primary/20",
      },
      {
        selected: false,
        variant: "surface",
        className:
          "text-muted hover:text-ink hover:bg-surface/50 font-medium",
      },
      {
        selected: true,
        variant: "subtle",
        className:
          "bg-primary-light text-primary border border-primary/30 font-bold shadow-2xs",
      },
      {
        selected: false,
        variant: "subtle",
        className:
          "text-muted hover:bg-surface-raised hover:text-ink",
      },
    ],
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "primary",
      shape: "rounded",
      size: "md",
      selected: false,
    },
  }
);

interface SegmentedControlContextValue {
  value: string;
  onValueChange: (val: string) => void;
  size: "sm" | "md" | "lg";
  shape: "rounded" | "pill";
  itemVariant: "primary" | "surface" | "subtle";
  fullWidth: boolean;
}

const SegmentedControlContext =
  React.createContext<SegmentedControlContextValue | null>(null);

function useSegmentedControl() {
  const ctx = React.useContext(SegmentedControlContext);
  if (!ctx) {
    throw new Error(
      "SegmentedControlItem deve ser usado dentro de um SegmentedControl"
    );
  }
  return ctx;
}

export interface SegmentedControlOption {
  value: string;
  label: React.ReactNode;
  detail?: string;
  icon?: React.ReactNode | React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export interface SegmentedControlProps
  extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>,
    VariantProps<typeof segmentedControlVariants> {
  value?: string;
  defaultValue?: string;
  onValueChange?: (value: string) => void;
  itemVariant?: "primary" | "surface" | "subtle";
  options?: SegmentedControlOption[];
}

export function SegmentedControl({
  className,
  variant,
  shape = "rounded",
  size = "md",
  fullWidth = false,
  itemVariant = "primary",
  value: controlledValue,
  defaultValue,
  onValueChange,
  options,
  children,
  ...props
}: SegmentedControlProps) {
  const [internalValue, setInternalValue] = React.useState(
    defaultValue ?? options?.[0]?.value ?? ""
  );

  const isControlled = controlledValue !== undefined;
  const activeValue = isControlled ? controlledValue : internalValue;

  const handleValueChange = React.useCallback(
    (newVal: string) => {
      if (!isControlled) {
        setInternalValue(newVal);
      }
      onValueChange?.(newVal);
    },
    [isControlled, onValueChange]
  );

  const contextValue = React.useMemo<SegmentedControlContextValue>(
    () => ({
      value: activeValue,
      onValueChange: handleValueChange,
      size: size ?? "md",
      shape: shape ?? "rounded",
      itemVariant: itemVariant ?? "primary",
      fullWidth: fullWidth ?? false,
    }),
    [activeValue, handleValueChange, size, shape, itemVariant, fullWidth]
  );

  return (
    <SegmentedControlContext.Provider value={contextValue}>
      <div
        role="tablist"
        className={cn(
          segmentedControlVariants({
            variant,
            shape,
            size,
            fullWidth,
          }),
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {options
          ? options.map((opt) => (
              <SegmentedControlItem
                key={opt.value}
                value={opt.value}
                disabled={opt.disabled}
                icon={opt.icon}
                detail={opt.detail}
              >
                {opt.label}
              </SegmentedControlItem>
            ))
          : children}
      </div>
    </SegmentedControlContext.Provider>
  );
}

export interface SegmentedControlItemProps
  extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  value: string;
  icon?: React.ReactNode | React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
  detail?: string;
}

export function SegmentedControlItem({
  value,
  icon,
  detail,
  className,
  children,
  disabled,
  onClick,
  ...props
}: SegmentedControlItemProps) {
  const {
    value: activeValue,
    onValueChange,
    size,
    shape,
    itemVariant,
    fullWidth,
  } = useSegmentedControl();

  const isSelected = activeValue === value;

  const handleClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
    if (disabled) return;
    onValueChange(value);
    onClick?.(e);
  };

  const renderIcon = () => {
    if (!icon) return null;
    if (React.isValidElement(icon)) return icon;
    const IconComponent = icon as React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
    const iconSizeClass =
      size === "sm" ? "size-3.5" : size === "lg" ? "size-4.5" : "size-4";
    return <IconComponent className={cn(iconSizeClass, "shrink-0")} />;
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      role="tab"
      aria-selected={isSelected}
      disabled={disabled}
      onClick={handleClick}
      className={cn(
        segmentedItemVariants({
          variant: itemVariant,
          shape,
          size,
          selected: isSelected,
        }),
        fullWidth && "flex-1",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {renderIcon()}
      {children && <span>{children}</span>}
      {detail && (
        <span
          className={cn(
            "text-[10px] transition-opacity",
            isSelected ? "opacity-90" : "opacity-60"
          )}
        >
          {detail}
        </span>
      )}
    </button>
  );
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/ui/switch-row.tsx
================================================================================

import * as React from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Switch } from "./switch";

export interface SwitchRowProps
  extends Omit<React.ComponentProps<"div">, "onChange"> {
  checked?: boolean;
  onCheckedChange?: (checked: boolean) => void;
  label: string;
  description?: string;
  icon?: React.ReactNode | React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
  iconClassName?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export function SwitchRow({
  checked = false,
  onCheckedChange,
  label,
  description,
  icon,
  iconClassName,
  disabled = false,
  className,
  ...props
}: SwitchRowProps) {
  const generatedId = React.useId();
  const switchId = props.id ?? generatedId;
  const descriptionId = `${switchId}-desc`;

  const handleToggle = () => {
    if (!disabled && onCheckedChange) {
      onCheckedChange(!checked);
    }
  };

  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (disabled) return;
    if (e.key === " " || e.key === "Enter") {
      e.preventDefault();
      handleToggle();
    }
  };

  const renderIcon = () => {
    if (!icon) return null;
    if (React.isValidElement(icon)) return icon;
    const IconComponent = icon as React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
    return <IconComponent className="size-4.5" />;
  };

  return (
    <div
      role="switch"
      tabIndex={disabled ? -1 : 0}
      aria-checked={checked}
      aria-label={label}
      aria-describedby={description ? descriptionId : undefined}
      aria-disabled={disabled}
      onClick={handleToggle}
      onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
      className={cn(
        "flex min-h-14 w-full items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface px-4 py-2.5 shadow-2xs select-none transition-all duration-200",
        disabled
          ? "cursor-not-allowed opacity-50"
          : "cursor-pointer hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-surface-raised/30 active:scale-[0.99] focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-primary",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        {icon && (
          <div
            className={cn(
              "flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-primary-light text-primary shadow-2xs",
              iconClassName
            )}
          >
            {renderIcon()}
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="flex flex-col">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink leading-snug">
            {label}
          </span>
          {description && (
            <span
              id={descriptionId}
              className="text-[10px] text-muted leading-relaxed"
            >
              {description}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="shrink-0 pointer-events-none">
        <Switch
          id={switchId}
          checked={checked}
          disabled={disabled}
          tabIndex={-1}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// Alias SettingRow para uso semântico em telas de ajustes
export const SettingRow = SwitchRow;


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/ui/switch.tsx
================================================================================

import * as React from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export interface SwitchProps {
    checked?: boolean;
    onCheckedChange?: (checked: boolean) => void;
    label?: string;
    description?: string;
    disabled?: boolean; 
    className?: string;
    id?: string;
    tabIndex?: number;
}

export function Switch({
  checked = false,
  onCheckedChange,
  label,
  description,
  disabled = false,
  className,
  id,
  tabIndex,
}: SwitchProps) {
  const handleClick = () => {
    if (!disabled && onCheckedChange) {
      onCheckedChange(!checked);
    }
  };

  const generatedId = React.useId();
  const switchId = id ?? generatedId;
  const descriptionId = `${switchId}-desc`;

  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
    if (e.key === " " || e.key === "Enter") {
      e.preventDefault();
      handleClick();
    }
  };

  const switchControl = (
    <div className="relative inline-flex min-h-12 min-w-12 items-center justify-center">
      <button
        id={switchId}
        type="button"
        role="switch"
        aria-checked={checked}
        aria-describedby={description ? descriptionId : undefined}
        disabled={disabled}
        tabIndex={tabIndex}
        onClick={handleClick}
        onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
        className={cn(
          "relative inline-flex h-7 w-12 shrink-0 cursor-pointer rounded-full p-0.5 transition-colors duration-200 ease-in-out",
          "focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-surface",
          "disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50",
          checked ? "bg-primary" : "bg-line",
          className
        )}
      >
        <span
          className={cn(
            "pointer-events-none inline-block size-6 rounded-full bg-surface shadow-md ring-0 transition-transform duration-200 ease-in-out",
            checked ? "translate-x-5" : "translate-x-0"
          )}
        />
      </button>
    </div>
  );

  if (!label && !description) {
    return switchControl;
  }

  return (
    <label
      htmlFor={switchId}
      className={cn(
        "flex min-h-12 items-center justify-between gap-4 cursor-pointer select-none rounded-xl p-1 transition-colors hover:bg-surface-raised/50",
        disabled && "cursor-not-allowed opacity-50"
      )}
    >
      <div className="flex flex-col">
        {label && (
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink leading-snug">
            {label}
          </span>
        )}
        {description && (
          <span id={descriptionId} className="text-[11px] text-muted leading-relaxed">
            {description}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      {switchControl}
    </label>
  );
}


================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components/ui/top-bar.tsx
================================================================================

import * as React from "react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export const topBarVariants = cva(
  "w-full shrink-0 transition-all duration-200 select-none",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        standard:
          "flex h-13 items-center justify-between border-b border-line/70 bg-surface-raised/80 px-1 backdrop-blur-sm",
        "large-title":
          "flex flex-col border-b border-line/70 bg-surface-raised/80 backdrop-blur-sm",
        contextual:
          "flex h-13 items-center justify-between border-b border-line/70 bg-primary/10 px-1 backdrop-blur-sm",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "standard",
    },
  }
);

export type TopBarVariant = NonNullable<
  VariantProps<typeof topBarVariants>["variant"]
>;

interface TopBarContextValue {
  variant: TopBarVariant;
}

const TopBarContext = React.createContext<TopBarContextValue>({
  variant: "standard",
});

export interface TopBarProps
  extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement>,
    VariantProps<typeof topBarVariants> {
  as?: "header" | "div" | "nav";
}

export function TopBar({
  className,
  variant = "standard",
  as: Component = "header",
  children,
  role = "banner",
  ...props
}: TopBarProps) {
  const resolvedVariant = variant ?? "standard";

  return (
    <TopBarContext.Provider value={{ variant: resolvedVariant }}>
      <Component
        role={role}
        className={cn(topBarVariants({ variant: resolvedVariant }), className)}
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
      </Component>
    </TopBarContext.Provider>
  );
}

export const topBarActionVariants = cva(
  "flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl transition-all cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "text-ink hover:bg-surface active:scale-90",
        muted: "text-muted hover:bg-surface hover:text-ink active:scale-90",
        primary: "text-primary hover:bg-surface active:scale-90",
        destructive: "text-red-500 hover:bg-red-500/10 active:scale-90",
      },
      size: {
        sm: "size-9",
        md: "size-10",
        lg: "size-11",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "md",
    },
  }
);

export interface TopBarActionProps
  extends React.ComponentProps<"button">,
    VariantProps<typeof topBarActionVariants> {
  icon?: React.ReactNode | React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
}

export function TopBarAction({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  size,
  icon,
  children,
  type = "button",
  ...props
}: TopBarActionProps) {
  const context = React.useContext(TopBarContext);
  const resolvedSize = size ?? (context.variant === "large-title" ? "sm" : "md");

  const renderIcon = () => {
    if (!icon) return null;
    if (React.isValidElement(icon)) {
      return icon;
    }
    const IconComponent = icon as React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
    const iconSizeClass = resolvedSize === "sm" ? "size-4" : "size-4.5";
    return <IconComponent className={iconSizeClass} />;
  };

  return (
    <button
      type={type}
      className={cn(
        topBarActionVariants({ variant, size: resolvedSize }),
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {renderIcon()}
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

export interface TopBarTitleProps extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement> {
  as?: "h1" | "h2" | "h3" | "span" | "p";
  variant?: "standard" | "large";
}

export function TopBarTitle({
  as,
  variant: titleVariant,
  className,
  children,
  ...props
}: TopBarTitleProps) {
  const context = React.useContext(TopBarContext);
  const isLarge =
    titleVariant === "large" || (!titleVariant && context.variant === "large-title");

  const Component = as ?? (isLarge ? "h2" : "span");

  return (
    <Component
      className={cn(
        isLarge
          ? "text-base font-extrabold tracking-tight text-ink"
          : "truncate px-2 text-xs font-bold text-ink",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </Component>
  );
}

export interface TopBarNavRowProps extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement> {
  as?: "header" | "div";
}

export function TopBarNavRow({
  as: Component = "header",
  className,
  ...props
}: TopBarNavRowProps) {
  return (
    <Component
      className={cn(
        "flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-between px-1",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export type TopBarLargeTitleAreaProps = React.ComponentProps<"div">;

export function TopBarLargeTitleArea({
  className,
  ...props
}: TopBarLargeTitleAreaProps) {
  return <div className={cn("px-3.5 pb-2", className)} {...props} />;
}

export interface TopBarSectionProps extends React.ComponentProps<"div"> {
  gap?: "tight" | "normal";
}

export function TopBarSection({
  className,
  gap = "tight",
  ...props
}: TopBarSectionProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "flex items-center",
        gap === "tight" ? "gap-0.5" : "gap-1",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export type TopBarActionsProps = React.ComponentProps<"div">;

export function TopBarActions({
  className,
  ...props
}: TopBarActionsProps) {
  return <div className={cn("flex items-center gap-0.5", className)} {...props} />;
}

================================================================================
ARQUIVO: lib/utils.ts
================================================================================

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx";
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge";

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
}
